```
## Complete Gossamer TUI Color Usage Analysis

I've conducted a thorough search of the gossamer codebase for all hardcoded color usage. Here's the complete picture:

### FILES USING COLORS (6 files)

1. **/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/show.rs** - Most extensive color usage
2. **/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/status.rs** - Session list rendering
3. **/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/sessions.rs** - Session browsing interface
4. **/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/search.rs** - Search results display
5. **/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/init.rs** - Agent selection UI
6. **/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/tidy.rs** - Stale worktree warnings

### COLOR PALETTE IN USE

All colors use **ANSI 256-color codes** with patterns:
- **Foreground**: `38;5;N` (e.g., `\x1b[38;5;240m`)
- **Background**: `48;5;N` (e.g., `\x1b[48;5;236m`)
- **Bold foreground**: `1;38;5;N` (e.g., `\x1b[1;38;5;229m`)

**Unique color values used (by frequency):**
- **38;5;240** (30 uses) - Dim gray, used for separators, timestamps, metadata
- **38;5;238** (10 uses) - Darker gray, used for secondary text
- **38;5;255** (4 uses) - Bright white
- **38;5;245** (4 uses) - Light gray
- **38;5;229** (4 uses) - Bright yellow/gold
- **38;5;82** (3 uses) - Bright green (active/running state)
- **38;5;239** (3 uses) - Dark gray (backed-up status)
- **38;5;214** (3 uses) - Orange (agent color for Claude)
- **38;5;220** (2 uses) - Yellow (branch labels)
- **38;5;75** (1 use) - Bright blue (Gemini agent, linked worktrees)
- **38;5;203** (1 use) - Red/salmon (warnings/tidy)
- **38;5;196** (1 use) - Red (detached state, errors)
- **38;5;177** (1 use) - Purple (non-error tool output)
- **38;5;241, 38;5;242** (1 use each) - Very dark grays
- **48;5;236** (7 uses as background) - Dark gray selection background

### AGENT COLOR MAPPING (Hardcoded Function)

**File**: `/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/mod.rs` (lines 17-27)

```
agent_color(name: &str) -> u8:
  "Claude"   → 214  (orange)
  "Copilot"  → 99   (purple)
  "Cursor"   → 33   (green)
  "Gemini"   → 75   (bright blue)
  "Aider"    → 42   (green)
  "ChatGPT"  → 35   (teal)
  "Windsurf" → 44   (teal)
  "Amazon Q" → 208  (orange-red)
  [default]  → 245  (light gray)
```

### EXISTING THEME/COLOR ABSTRACTION

**Markdown rendering with termimad** (`show.rs` lines 410-442):
- Uses `termimad::MadSkin` with crossterm Color::AnsiValue for markdown styling
- Bold: 255, Italic: 252, Inline code: 116, Code blocks: 116
- Headers: 229 (h1), 222 (h2), 216 (h3)
- Paragraphs: 252, Bullets: 252
- This is the **only structured color abstraction** - used for rendering markdown in session details

### KEY CHARACTERISTICS

1. **No global theme system** - Colors are hardcoded directly in format! strings throughout the codebase
2. **No theme constants** - Only two local constants defined: `SEL_BG = "48;5;236"` (repeated in 5 files)
3. **Pattern consistency**: Semantic use of colors (gray for metadata, green for active, red for errors)
4. **ANSI escape codes only** - Uses raw `\x1b[...m` sequences, no crossterm::style abstractions except in markdown rendering
5. **Agent-specific coloring** - Central function (`agent_color`) provides some abstraction for agent names

### COLOR BY SEMANTIC USE

- **Metadata/Time** (240): timestamps, relative times, decorative separators
- **Subdued text** (238, 245): secondary information, truncation indicators
- **Highlights** (255): important field values, selected items
- **Status indicators**:
  - Green (82): active/running/current
  - Orange (214): Claude agent, time thresholds
  - Blue (75): Gemini agent, linked worktrees
  - Red (196): detached state, errors
  - Gold (229, 220): headers, branch labels
- **Selection** (48;5;236 background): interactive selection backgrounds
```

```
12	#[command(name = "gossamer", about = "Manage AI sessions with entireio")]
13	struct Cli {
14	    #[command(subcommand)]
15	    command: Option<Commands>,
16	
17	    /// Output as JSON instead of an interactive TUI

22	#[derive(Subcommand)]
23	enum Commands {
24	    /// Initialize gossamer in the current git repository
25	    Init,
26	    /// Print all repositories tracked by gossamer
27	    Repo,
28	    /// Print sessions from the past 3 days
29	    Sessions {
30	        /// Show all sessions regardless of age
31	        #[arg(long)]
32	        all: bool,
33	    },
34	    /// Scan checkpoint logs and index sessions into the database
35	    Index,
36	    /// Incrementally index only new checkpoint commits since the last index/refresh
37	    Refresh,
38	    /// Browse a session's messages interactively (arrow keys to navigate)
39	    Show {
40	        /// Session ID or path to a JSONL file
41	        session: String,
42	    },
43	    /// Semantic search across indexed sessions
44	    Search {
45	        /// Search query (separate words are joined into one query)
46	        #[arg(required = true)]
47	        query: Vec<String>,
48	        /// Number of results to return
49	        #[arg(short = 'n', long, default_value = "10")]
50	        top_k: usize,
51	    },
52	    /// Start a new AI coding session, optionally in a fresh git worktree
53	    NewSession {
54	        /// Agent to use: claude, gemini, aider [default: claude]
55	        #[arg(long, short = 'a')]
56	        agent: Option<String>,
57	        /// Create a new git worktree on this branch before launching
58	        #[arg(long, short = 'b')]
59	        branch: Option<String>,
60	        /// Session name (passed as -n to the agent)
61	        #[arg(long, short = 'n')]
62	        name: Option<String>,
63	        /// Initial prompt, also copied to clipboard
64	        prompt: Option<String>,
65	    },
66	    /// Resume a session, checking out a new worktree if needed
67	    Resume {
68	        /// Session ID (full UUID or unambiguous prefix)
69	        session_id: String,
70	    },
71	    /// Remove a session: runs `entire clean`, deletes from DB, removes search index entries
72	    Clean {
73	        /// Session ID to clean up
74	        session_id: String,
75	    },
76	    /// Remove git worktrees that have had no recent session activity
77	    Tidy {
78	        /// Only list stale worktrees without removing them
79	        #[arg(long)]
80	        dry_run: bool,
81	        /// Days of inactivity before a worktree is considered stale
82	        #[arg(long, default_value = "7")]
83	        days: i64,
84	        /// Pass --force to git worktree remove (removes worktrees with uncommitted changes)
85	        #[arg(long)]
86	        force: bool,
87	        /// Also remove sessions whose cwd was inside each removed worktree
88	        #[arg(long)]
89	        sessions: bool,
90	    },
91	    /// Attach an existing session with entireio and index it into witchcraft
92	    Attach {
93	        /// Session ID to attach
94	        session_id: String,
95	        /// Agent name passed to `entire attach`
96	        #[arg(short, long, default_value = "claude-code")]
97	        agent: String,
98	        /// Pass --force to `entire attach`
99	        #[arg(short, long)]
100	        force: bool,
101	    },
102	    /// Push a session's checkpoint to the remote so another agent can resume it
103	    Handoff {
104	        /// Session ID (full UUID or unambiguous prefix)
105	        session_id: String,
106	        /// Force push even if the remote has diverged
107	        #[arg(short, long)]
108	        force: bool,
109	    },
110	    /// Show or set gossamer configuration
111	    Config {
112	        /// Path to the witchcraft assets directory (enables semantic search)
113	        assets: Option<String>,
114	    },
115	    /// Called by the Claude Code SessionStart hook — reads JSON from stdin
116	    #[command(hide = true)]
117	    SessionStart,
118	    /// Called by the Claude Code Stop hook — ingests the finished session
119	    #[command(hide = true)]
120	    SessionStop,
121	}
122	
123	fn main() -> Result<()> {
124	    let cli = Cli::parse();
125	    let json = cli.json;
126	    match cli.command.unwrap_or(Commands::Repo) {
127	        Commands::Init => commands::init::run(json)?,
128	        Commands::Repo => commands::status::run(json)?,
129	        Commands::Sessions { all } => commands::sessions::run(all, json)?,
130	        Commands::Index => commands::index::run(json)?,
131	        Commands::Refresh => commands::refresh::run(json)?,
132	        Commands::Show { session } => commands::show::run(&session)?,
133	        Commands::Search { query, top_k } => commands::search::run(&query.join(" "), top_k, json)?,
134	        Commands::NewSession { agent, branch, name, prompt } => {
135	            commands::new_session::run(agent, branch, name, prompt)?
136	        }
137	        Commands::Resume { session_id } => commands::resume::run(&session_id)?,
138	        Commands::Clean { session_id } => commands::clean::run(&session_id, json)?,
139	        Commands::Tidy { dry_run, days, force, sessions } => commands::tidy::run(days, dry_run, force, sessions, json)?,
140	        Commands::Attach { session_id, agent, force } => commands::attach::run(&session_id, &agent, force, json)?,
141	        Commands::Handoff { session_id, force } => commands::handoff::run(&session_id, force, json)?,
142	        Commands::Config { assets: Some(path) } => config::set_warp_assets(&path)?,
143	        Commands::Config { assets: None }       => config::show(),
144	        Commands::SessionStart => commands::session_start::run()?,
145	        Commands::SessionStop  => commands::session_stop::run()?,
146	    }
147	    Ok(())
148	}
149
```

```
49	// ── Entry point ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

51	pub fn run(json: bool) -> Result<()> {
52	    let conn = db::connect()?;
53	    let repos = fetch_repos(&conn)?;
54	
55	    if json {
56	        let arr: Vec<serde_json::Value> = repos.iter().map(|r| {
57	            let worktrees: Vec<serde_json::Value> = fetch_worktrees(&r.directory).into_iter().map(|wt| serde_json::json!({
58	                "path": wt.path,
59	                "branch": wt.branch,
60	                "head": wt.head,
61	                "is_main": wt.is_main,
62	            })).collect();
63	            let sessions: Vec<serde_json::Value> = load_sessions(&r.directory).into_iter().map(|s| serde_json::json!({
64	                "session_id": s.session_id,
65	                "session_name": s.session_name,
66	                "branch": s.branch,
67	                "agent": s.agent,
68	                "updated_at": s.updated_at.to_rfc3339(),
69	                "backed_up": s.backed_up,
70	            })).collect();
71	            serde_json::json!({
72	                "name": r.name,
73	                "directory": r.directory,
74	                "remote": r.remote,
75	                "worktrees": worktrees,
76	                "sessions": sessions,
77	            })
78	        }).collect();
79	        println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&serde_json::json!({ "repos": arr }))?);
80	        return Ok(());
81	    }
82	
83	    if repos.is_empty() {
84	        println!("No repositories tracked. Run `gossamer init` in a git repo to get started.");
85	        return Ok(());
86	    }
87	
88	    // The shell wrapper sets GOSSAMER_CDPATH to a temp file path.
89	    // We write the selected directory there instead of stdout, avoiding all
90	    // the stdout-capture / /dev/tty complexity.
91	    let cd_file = std::env::var("GOSSAMER_CDPATH").ok();
92	
93	    if !io::stdout().is_terminal() && cd_file.is_none() {
94	        // Plain list when truly non-interactive (piped, no wrapper)
95	        let cwd = env::current_dir().ok().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());
96	        let current_dir = cwd.as_deref().and_then(|cwd| {
97	            repos.iter().find(|r| cwd.starts_with(r.directory.as_str())).map(|r| r.directory.as_str())
98	        });
99	        for repo in &repos {
100	            let is_cur = current_dir == Some(repo.directory.as_str());
101	            let dot = if is_cur { "*" } else { " " };
102	            println!("{dot} {}  {}  {}", repo.name, repo.directory, repo.remote);
103	        }
104	        return Ok(());
105	    }
106	
107	    // Register panic hook to restore terminal
108	    let orig_hook = std::panic::take_hook();
109	    std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |info| {
110	        let mut out = io::stdout();
111	        let _ = execute!(out, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show);
112	        let _ = terminal::disable_raw_mode();
113	        orig_hook(info);
114	    }));
115	
116	    let mut stdout = io::stdout();
117	    terminal::enable_raw_mode()?;
118	    execute!(stdout, EnterAlternateScreen, cursor::Hide)?;
119	
120	    let outcome = tui_loop(&mut stdout, &repos, cd_file.is_some(), None);
121	
122	    execute!(stdout, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show)?;
123	    terminal::disable_raw_mode()?;
124	
125	    match outcome? {
126	        Some(TuiOutcome::CdTo(path)) => {
127	            if let Some(file) = cd_file {
128	                std::fs::write(&file, &path)?;
129	            }
130	        }
131	        Some(TuiOutcome::ResumeSession { id, cwd }) => {
132	            super::show::resume_session(&id, &cwd);
133	        }
134	        Some(TuiOutcome::LaunchAgent { cli, cwd, session_name, prompt }) => {
135	            use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
136	            let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(&cli);
137	            if !cwd.is_empty() { cmd.current_dir(&cwd); }
138	            if !session_name.is_empty() { cmd.args(["-n", &session_name]); }
139	            if !prompt.is_empty() { cmd.arg(&prompt); }
140	            let err = cmd.exec();
141	            eprintln!("Failed to launch {cli}: {err}");
142	        }
143	        None => {}
144	    }
145	
146	    Ok(())
147	}
148	
149	enum TuiOutcome {
150	    CdTo(String),
151	    ResumeSession { id: String, cwd: String },
152	    LaunchAgent { cli: String, cwd: String, session_name: String, prompt: String },
153	}
154	
155	enum Screen {
156	    Repos { sel: usize },
157	    Sessions { repo_idx: usize, sel: usize, sessions: Vec<RepoSession>, worktrees: Vec<RepoWorktree> },
158	}
159	
160	fn tui_loop(stdout: &mut impl Write, repos: &[Repository], has_cd: bool, start_repo: Option<usize>) -> Result<Option<TuiOutcome>> {
161	    let cwd = env::current_dir().ok().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());
162	    let current_repo_dir = cwd.as_deref().and_then(|cwd| {
163	        repos.iter().find(|r| cwd.starts_with(r.directory.as_str())).map(|r| r.directory.as_str())
164	    });
165	
166	    let start_sel = cwd.as_deref()
167	        .and_then(|cwd| repos.iter().position(|r| cwd.starts_with(r.directory.as_str())))
168	        .unwrap_or(0);
169	
170	    let initial = if let Some(idx) = start_repo.filter(|&i| i < repos.len()) {
171	        let sessions = load_sessions(&repos[idx].directory);
172	        let worktrees = fetch_worktrees(&repos[idx].directory);
173	        Screen::Sessions { repo_idx: idx, sel: 0, sessions, worktrees }
174	    } else {
175	        Screen::Repos { sel: start_sel }
176	    };
177	
178	    // Navigation stack — back pops; when empty the TUI exits.
179	    let mut stack: Vec<Screen> = vec![initial];
180	
181	    // Commands produced inside match arms, executed after the borrow ends.
182	    enum Cmd {
183	        None,
184	        Break,
185	        Back,
186	        PushSessions(usize),
187	        Cd(String),
188	        ShowSession(String),
189	        ResumeSession(String, String), // (session_id, repo_dir)
190	        Search(String),
191	        NewWorktree(String), // branch name
192	        NewSession(NewSessionConfig),
193	        Tidy,
194	        Redraw,
195	    }
196	
197	    loop {
198	        let (w, h) = terminal::size().unwrap_or((120, 40));
199	        let w = w as usize;
200	        let h = h as usize;
201	
202	        match stack.last().unwrap() {
203	            Screen::Repos { sel } => draw_repos(stdout, repos, *sel, current_repo_dir, w, h, has_cd)?,
204	            Screen::Sessions { repo_idx, sel, sessions, worktrees } => {
205	                draw_sessions(stdout, repos, *repo_idx, sessions, worktrees, *sel, w, h)?
206	            }
207	        }
208	
209	        let cmd = match event::read()? {
210	            Event::Key(k) => match stack.last_mut().unwrap() {
211	                Screen::Repos { sel } => match k.code {
212	                    KeyCode::Char('q') | KeyCode::Esc => Cmd::Break,
213	                    KeyCode::Char('c') if k.modifiers.contains(crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::CONTROL) => {
214	                        execute!(stdout, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show).ok();
215	                        terminal::disable_raw_mode().ok();
216	                        std::process::exit(0);
217	                    }
218	                    KeyCode::Up   | KeyCode::Char('k') => { if *sel > 0 { *sel -= 1; } Cmd::None }
219	                    KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => { if *sel + 1 < repos.len() { *sel += 1; } Cmd::None }
220	                    KeyCode::Char('g') => { *sel = 0; Cmd::None }
221	                    KeyCode::Char('G') => { *sel = repos.len().saturating_sub(1); Cmd::None }
222	                    KeyCode::Char(' ') | KeyCode::Right => Cmd::PushSessions(*sel),
223	                    KeyCode::Char('c') if has_cd => Cmd::Cd(repos[*sel].directory.clone()),
224	                    KeyCode::Char('s') => {
225	                        let repo_dir = repos[*sel].directory.clone();
226	                        match new_session_wizard(stdout, &repo_dir, w, h) {
227	                            Some(cfg) => Cmd::NewSession(cfg),
228	                            None => Cmd::Redraw,
229	                        }
230	                    }
231	                    KeyCode::Char('/') => match collect_search_query(stdout, w, h) {
232	                        Some(q) if !q.trim().is_empty() => Cmd::Search(q),
233	                        _ => Cmd::Redraw,
234	                    },
235	                    _ => Cmd::None,
236	                },
237	                Screen::Sessions { sel, sessions, repo_idx, .. } => match k.code {
238	                    KeyCode::Char('q') => Cmd::Break,
239	                    KeyCode::Char('c') if k.modifiers.contains(crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::CONTROL) => {
240	                        execute!(stdout, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show).ok();
241	                        terminal::disable_raw_mode().ok();
242	                        std::process::exit(0);
243	                    }
244	                    KeyCode::Esc | KeyCode::Char('h') | KeyCode::Left => Cmd::Back,
245	                    KeyCode::Up   | KeyCode::Char('k') => { if *sel > 0 { *sel -= 1; } Cmd::None }
246	                    KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => { if *sel + 1 < sessions.len() { *sel += 1; } Cmd::None }
247	                    KeyCode::Char('g') => { *sel = 0; Cmd::None }
248	                    KeyCode::Char('G') => { *sel = sessions.len().saturating_sub(1); Cmd::None }
249	                    KeyCode::Char(' ') | KeyCode::Right => {
250	                        if sessions.is_empty() { Cmd::None }
251	                        else { Cmd::ShowSession(sessions[*sel].session_id.clone()) }
252	                    }
253	                    KeyCode::Char('r') => {
254	                        if sessions.is_empty() { Cmd::None }
255	                        else { Cmd::ResumeSession(sessions[*sel].session_id.clone(), repos[*repo_idx].directory.clone()) }
256	                    }
257	                    KeyCode::Char('/') => match collect_search_query(stdout, w, h) {
258	                        Some(q) if !q.trim().is_empty() => Cmd::Search(q),
259	                        _ => Cmd::Redraw,
260	                    },
261	                    KeyCode::Char('n') => match collect_text_input(stdout, "  new worktree branch: ", w, h) {
262	                        Some(b) if !b.trim().is_empty() => Cmd::NewWorktree(b.trim().to_string()),
263	                        _ => Cmd::Redraw,
264	                    },
265	                    KeyCode::Char('s') => {
266	                        let repo_dir = repos[*repo_idx].directory.clone();
267	                        match new_session_wizard(stdout, &repo_dir, w, h) {
268	                            Some(cfg) => Cmd::NewSession(cfg),
269	                            None => Cmd::Redraw,
270	                        }
271	                    }
272	                    KeyCode::Char('t') => Cmd::Tidy,
273	                    _ => Cmd::None,
274	                },
275	            },
276	            Event::Resize(_, _) => Cmd::Redraw,
277	            _ => Cmd::None,
278	        };
279	
280	        match cmd {
281	            Cmd::None => {}
282	            Cmd::Break => break,
283	            Cmd::Back => {
284	                stack.pop();
285	                if stack.is_empty() { break; }
286	            }
287	            Cmd::PushSessions(idx) => {
288	                let sessions = load_sessions(&repos[idx].directory);
289	                let worktrees = fetch_worktrees(&repos[idx].directory);
290	                stack.push(Screen::Sessions { repo_idx: idx, sel: 0, sessions, worktrees });
291	            }
292	            Cmd::Cd(path) => return Ok(Some(TuiOutcome::CdTo(path))),
293	            Cmd::ResumeSession(id, cwd) => return Ok(Some(TuiOutcome::ResumeSession { id, cwd })),
294	            Cmd::ShowSession(id) => {
295	                execute!(stdout, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show).ok();
296	                terminal::disable_raw_mode().ok();
297	                let _ = super::show::run(&id);
298	                terminal::enable_raw_mode().ok();
299	                execute!(stdout, EnterAlternateScreen, cursor::Hide).ok();
300	                execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::All)).ok();
301	            }
302	            Cmd::Search(query) => {
303	                execute!(stdout, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show).ok();
304	                terminal::disable_raw_mode().ok();
305	                let _ = super::search::run(&query, 10, false);
306	                terminal::enable_raw_mode().ok();
307	                execute!(stdout, EnterAlternateScreen, cursor::Hide).ok();
308	                execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::All)).ok();
309	            }
310	            Cmd::NewWorktree(branch) => {
311	                if let Some(Screen::Sessions { repo_idx, worktrees, .. }) = stack.last_mut() {
312	                    let repo_dir = &repos[*repo_idx].directory;
313	                    let notification = create_worktree(repo_dir, &branch);
314	                    // Refresh the worktree list whether or not it succeeded.
315	                    *worktrees = fetch_worktrees(repo_dir);
316	                    // Show the result in the status bar until the next keypress.
317	                    let (tw, th) = terminal::size().unwrap_or((120, 40));
318	                    let msg: String = notification.chars().take(tw as usize).collect();
319	                    let padded = format!("{:<width$}", msg, width = tw as usize);
320	                    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, th - 1)).ok();
321	                    write!(stdout, "\x1b[7m{padded}\x1b[0m").ok();
322	                    stdout.flush().ok();
323	                }
324	            }
325	            Cmd::NewSession(cfg) => {
326	                // Create worktree if requested, then launch the agent.
327	                let launch_dir = if let Some(ref branch) = cfg.branch {
328	                    let _ = create_worktree(&cfg.repo_dir, branch);
329	                    if let Some(Screen::Sessions { repo_idx, worktrees, .. }) = stack.last_mut() {
330	                        *worktrees = fetch_worktrees(&repos[*repo_idx].directory);
331	                    }
332	                    let repo_path = std::path::Path::new(&cfg.repo_dir);
333	                    let parent = repo_path.parent().unwrap_or(repo_path);
334	                    let repo_name = repo_path.file_name()
335	                        .map(|s| s.to_string_lossy().to_string())
336	                        .unwrap_or_else(|| "repo".to_string());
337	                    parent.join(format!("{repo_name}-{}", branch)).to_string_lossy().to_string()
338	                } else {
339	                    cfg.repo_dir.clone()
340	                };
341	
342	                // Copy prompt to clipboard as a convenience.
343	                let prompt = cfg.prompt.trim().to_string();
344	                if !prompt.is_empty() {
345	                    if let Ok(mut child) = std::process::Command::new("pbcopy")
346	                        .stdin(std::process::Stdio::piped())
347	                        .spawn()
348	                    {
349	                        if let Some(stdin) = child.stdin.as_mut() {
350	                            let _ = stdin.write_all(prompt.as_bytes());
351	                        }
352	                        let _ = child.wait();
353	                    }
354	                }
355	
356	                let session_name = cfg.session_name.trim().to_string();
357	                return Ok(Some(TuiOutcome::LaunchAgent {
358	                    cli: cfg.agent_cli,
359	                    cwd: launch_dir,
360	                    session_name,
361	                    prompt,
362	                }));
363	            }
364	            Cmd::Tidy => {
365	                let repo_idx_opt = if let Some(Screen::Sessions { repo_idx, .. }) = stack.last() {
366	                    Some(*repo_idx)
367	                } else {
368	                    None
369	                };
370	                if let Some(repo_idx) = repo_idx_opt {
371	                    let repo = &repos[repo_idx];
372	                    let (tw, th) = terminal::size().unwrap_or((120, 40));
373	                    let changed = super::tidy::tui_tidy(
374	                        stdout,
375	                        std::slice::from_ref(repo),
376	                        7,
377	                        false,
378	                        tw as usize,
379	                        th as usize,
380	                    );
381	                    if changed {
382	                        if let Some(Screen::Sessions { worktrees, .. }) = stack.last_mut() {
383	                            *worktrees = fetch_worktrees(&repos[repo_idx].directory);
384	                        }
385	                    }
386	                    execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::All)).ok();
387	                }
388	            }
389	            Cmd::Redraw => { execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::All)).ok(); }
390	        }
391	    }
392	
393	    Ok(None)
394	}
395	
396	// ── Renderers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
397	
398	fn draw_repos(
399	    stdout: &mut impl Write,
400	    repos: &[Repository],
401	    sel: usize,
402	    current_repo_dir: Option<&str>,
403	    w: usize,
404	    h: usize,
405	    has_cd: bool,
406	) -> io::Result<()> {
407	    const SEL_BG: &str = "48;5;236";
408	
409	    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, 0))?;
410	
411	    let content_h = h.saturating_sub(1);
412	    let mut row = 0usize;
413	
414	    let name_w = repos.iter().map(|r| r.name.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);
415	    let dir_w  = repos.iter().map(|r| r.directory.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);
416	
417	    for (i, repo) in repos.iter().enumerate() {
418	        if row >= content_h { break }
419	
420	        let is_sel = i == sel;
421	        let is_cur = current_repo_dir == Some(repo.directory.as_str());
422	        let dot_col = if is_cur { "38;5;82" } else { "38;5;240" };
423	
424	        let name_padded = format!("{:<name_w$}", repo.name);
425	        let dir_padded  = format!("{:<dir_w$}",  repo.directory);
426	        let line = format!(
427	            "\x1b[{dot_col}m*\x1b[0m \x1b[38;5;255m{name_padded}\x1b[0m  \x1b[38;5;240m{dir_padded}  {}\x1b[0m",
428	            repo.remote
429	        );
430	
431	        print_row(stdout, &line, is_sel, SEL_BG, w, row as u16)?;
432	        row += 1;
433	    }
434	
435	    // Clear remaining rows
436	    while row < content_h {
437	        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16), terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
438	        row += 1;
439	    }
440	
441	    // Status bar
442	    let cd_hint = if has_cd { "   c: cd" } else { "" };
443	    let bar = format!(
444	        "  {} repos   ↑↓/jk navigate   space: sessions   s: new session   /: search{}   q: quit  ",
445	        repos.len(), cd_hint
446	    );
447	    draw_status(stdout, &bar, w, h)?;
448	
449	    stdout.flush()
450	}
451	
452	fn draw_sessions(
453	    stdout: &mut impl Write,
454	    repos: &[Repository],
455	    repo_idx: usize,
456	    sessions: &[RepoSession],
457	    worktrees: &[RepoWorktree],
458	    sel: usize,
459	    w: usize,
460	    h: usize,
461	) -> io::Result<()> {
462	    const SEL_BG: &str = "48;5;236";
463	
464	    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, 0))?;
465	    write!(stdout, "\x1b[1;38;5;229m{}\x1b[0m", repos[repo_idx].name)?;
466	    execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
467	
468	    let content_h = h.saturating_sub(2); // header + status bar
469	    let mut row = 1usize;
470	
471	    // ── Worktrees ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
472	    if !worktrees.is_empty() {
473	        for wt in worktrees.iter().take(content_h.saturating_sub(row + 1)) {
474	            if row >= content_h { break; }
475	
476	            let (branch_col, branch_label) = if wt.branch == "(detached)" {
477	                ("38;5;196", format!("detached:{}", &wt.head))
478	            } else if wt.is_main {
479	                ("38;5;229", wt.branch.clone())   // gold — main worktree
480	            } else {
481	                ("38;5;75",  wt.branch.clone())   // blue — linked worktree
482	            };
483	
484	            let path_short = short_path_s(&wt.path);
485	            let line = format!(
486	                "\x1b[38;5;240m  @ \x1b[{branch_col}m{branch_label}\x1b[0m  \x1b[38;5;240m{path_short}\x1b[0m"
487	            );
488	            execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16))?;
489	            write!(stdout, "{line}")?;
490	            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
491	            row += 1;
492	        }
493	
494	        // Blank separator between worktrees and sessions
495	        if row < content_h {
496	            execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16), terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
497	            row += 1;
498	        }
499	    }
500	
501	    // ── Sessions ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
502	    let sessions_start = row;
503	    let sessions_h = content_h.saturating_sub(sessions_start);
504	    let scroll = if sel >= sessions_h { sel + 1 - sessions_h } else { 0 };
505	
506	    if sessions.is_empty() {
507	        if row < content_h {
508	            execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16))?;
509	            write!(stdout, "\x1b[38;5;240m  no sessions found\x1b[0m")?;
510	            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
511	            row += 1;
512	        }
513	    } else {
514	        // Pre-compute column widths for table alignment
515	        let name_w   = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.session_name.trim().chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0).min(40);
516	        let branch_w = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.branch.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);
517	        let agent_w  = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.agent.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);
518	
519	        for (i, s) in sessions.iter().enumerate().skip(scroll) {
520	            if row >= content_h { break; }
521	
522	            let id_short: String = s.session_id.chars().take(8).collect();
523	            let ts = relative_time(s.updated_at);
524	            let name: String = s.session_name.trim().chars().take(name_w).collect();
525	            let age = (Utc::now() - s.updated_at).num_seconds().max(0);
526	            let dot_col = match age {
527	                a if a < 900   => "38;5;82",
528	                a if a < 3_600 => "38;5;214",
529	                _              => "38;5;240",
530	            };
531	
532	            let (name_col, meta_col, dot_char) = if s.backed_up {
533	                ("1;38;5;255", "38;5;240", "*")
534	            } else {
535	                ("38;5;242", "38;5;238", "·")
536	            };
537	            let branch_col = if s.backed_up { "38;5;75" } else { "38;5;239" };
538	
539	            let name_padded = format!("{:<name_w$}", name);
540	            let mut line = format!("\x1b[{dot_col}m{dot_char}\x1b[0m \x1b[{name_col}m{name_padded}\x1b[0m");
541	
542	            if branch_w > 0 {
543	                let b: String = s.branch.chars().take(branch_w).collect();
544	                let pad = " ".repeat(branch_w - b.chars().count());
545	                line.push_str(&format!("  \x1b[{branch_col}m{b}{pad}\x1b[0m"));
546	            }
547	
548	            if agent_w > 0 {
549	                let col = if s.backed_up { agent_color(&s.agent) } else { 239 };
550	                let a: String = s.agent.chars().take(agent_w).collect();
551	                let pad = " ".repeat(agent_w - a.chars().count());
552	                line.push_str(&format!("  \x1b[38;5;{col}m{a}{pad}\x1b[0m"));
553	            }
554	
555	            line.push_str(&format!("  \x1b[{meta_col}m{id_short}  {ts}\x1b[0m"));
556	
557	            print_row(stdout, &line, i == sel, SEL_BG, w, row as u16)?;
558	            row += 1;
559	        }
560	    }
561	
562	    while row < content_h {
563	        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16), terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
564	        row += 1;
565	    }
566	
567	    let bar = format!(
568	        "  {} sessions   ↑↓/jk navigate   space: view   r: resume   s: new session   n: new worktree   t: tidy   /: search   ←/h: back   q: quit  ",
569	        sessions.len()
570	    );
571	    draw_status(stdout, &bar, w, h)?;
572	
573	    stdout.flush()
574	}
575	
576	fn print_row(stdout: &mut impl Write, line: &str, selected: bool, bg: &str, w: usize, row: u16) -> io::Result<()> {
577	    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row))?;
578	    if selected {
579	        let colored = with_bg(line, bg);
580	        let vis = visible_width(line);
581	        let pad = w.saturating_sub(vis);
582	        write!(stdout, "\x1b[{bg}m{colored}{}\x1b[0m", " ".repeat(pad))?;
583	    } else {
584	        write!(stdout, "{line}")?;
585	        execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
586	    }
587	    Ok(())
588	}
589	
590	fn draw_status(stdout: &mut impl Write, bar: &str, w: usize, h: usize) -> io::Result<()> {
591	    let display: String = bar.chars().take(w).collect();
592	    let padded = format!("{:<width$}", display, width = w);
593	    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (h - 1) as u16))?;
594	    write!(stdout, "\x1b[7m{padded}\x1b[0m")
595	}
596	
597	// ── Worktree loading ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
598	
599	fn fetch_worktrees(repo_dir: &str) -> Vec<RepoWorktree> {
600	    let out = std::process::Command::new("git")
601	        .args(["worktree", "list", "--porcelain"])
602	        .current_dir(repo_dir)
603	        .output();
604	
605	    let Ok(out) = out else { return vec![]; };
606	    if !out.status.success() { return vec![]; }
607	    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
608	
609	    let mut result = Vec::new();
610	    let mut first = true;
611	
612	    // Each worktree block is separated by a blank line.
613	    for block in text.split("\n\n") {
614	        let mut path = String::new();
615	        let mut head = String::new();
616	        let mut branch = String::new();
617	        let mut detached = false;
618	
619	        for line in block.lines() {
620	            if let Some(v) = line.strip_prefix("worktree ") {
621	                path = v.to_string();
622	            } else if let Some(v) = line.strip_prefix("HEAD ") {
623	                head = v.chars().take(8).collect();
624	            } else if let Some(v) = line.strip_prefix("branch refs/heads/") {
625	                branch = v.to_string();
626	            } else if line == "detached" {
627	                detached = true;
628	            }
629	        }
630	
631	        if path.is_empty() { continue; }
632	        if detached { branch = "(detached)".to_string(); }
633	        if branch.is_empty() { continue; } // bare worktree — skip
634	
635	        result.push(RepoWorktree { path, branch, head, is_main: first });
636	        first = false;
637	    }
638	
639	    result
640	}
641	
642	// ── Session loading ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
643	
644	fn load_sessions(cwd_prefix: &str) -> Vec<RepoSession> {
645	    let mut sessions: Vec<RepoSession> = Vec::new();
646	
647	    // From DB
648	    if let Ok(conn) = db::connect() {
649	        if let Ok(mut stmt) = conn.prepare(
650	            "SELECT session_id, session_name, updated_at, agent_name FROM sessions WHERE cwd LIKE ?1 ORDER BY updated_at DESC"
651	        ) {
652	            let prefix_pattern = format!("{cwd_prefix}%");
653	            let _ = stmt.query_map([&prefix_pattern], |row| {
654	                let ts_str: String = row.get(2)?;
655	                let updated_at = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&ts_str)
656	                    .map(|dt| dt.with_timezone(&Utc))
657	                    .unwrap_or_else(|_| Utc::now());
658	                Ok(RepoSession {
659	                    session_id: row.get(0)?,
660	                    session_name: row.get(1)?,
661	                    updated_at,
662	                    branch: String::new(),
663	                    agent: row.get(3)?,
664	                    backed_up: true,
665	                })
666	            }).map(|rows| {
667	                for r in rows.flatten() { sessions.push(r); }
668	            });
669	        }
670	    }
671	
672	    // From ~/.claude/projects/ — refresh existing DB sessions and add new ones
673	    if let Ok(home) = std::env::var("HOME") {
674	        let projects = PathBuf::from(&home).join(".claude/projects");
675	        if let Ok(dirs) = std::fs::read_dir(&projects) {
676	            for dir_entry in dirs.flatten() {
677	                let dir = dir_entry.path();
678	                if !dir.is_dir() { continue }
679	                if let Ok(files) = std::fs::read_dir(&dir) {
680	                    for f in files.flatten() {
681	                        let path = f.path();
682	                        if path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) != Some("jsonl") { continue }
683	                        let session_id = match path.file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) {
684	                            Some(s) => s.to_string(),
685	                            None => continue,
686	                        };
687	
688	                        let file_mtime = f.metadata().ok()
689	                            .and_then(|m| m.modified().ok())
690	                            .map(DateTime::<Utc>::from)
691	                            .unwrap_or_else(Utc::now);
692	
693	                        let Ok(file) = std::fs::File::open(&path) else { continue };
694	                        let reader = std::io::BufReader::new(file);
695	                        let mut session_name = String::new();
696	                        let mut cwd_found = String::new();
697	                        let mut last_prompt = String::new();
698	                        let mut git_branch = String::new();
699	
700	                        for line in reader.lines().flatten() {
701	                            let Ok(v) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&line) else { continue };
702	                            match v["type"].as_str() {
703	                                Some("custom-title") => {
704	                                    if let Some(t) = v["customTitle"].as_str() { session_name = t.to_string(); }
705	                                }
706	                                Some("user") | Some("system") => {
707	                                    if cwd_found.is_empty() {
708	                                        if let Some(c) = v["cwd"].as_str() { cwd_found = c.to_string(); }
709	                                    }
710	                                }
711	                                _ => {}
712	                            }
713	                            if v["type"].as_str() == Some("user") {
714	                                if let Some(t) = user_text(&v["message"]["content"]) {
715	                                    last_prompt = t;
716	                                }
717	                            }
718	                            // Track the most recent branch seen in this session
719	                            if let Some(b) = v["gitBranch"].as_str() {
720	                                if !b.is_empty() { git_branch = b.to_string(); }
721	                            }
722	                        }
723	
724	                        let display_name = if !session_name.is_empty() { session_name } else { last_prompt };
725	
726	                        if let Some(existing) = sessions.iter_mut().find(|s| s.session_id == session_id) {
727	                            // Refresh the name, timestamp, and branch from live JSONL
728	                            if !display_name.is_empty() { existing.session_name = display_name; }
729	                            if file_mtime > existing.updated_at { existing.updated_at = file_mtime; }
730	                            if !git_branch.is_empty() { existing.branch = git_branch; }
731	                            continue;
732	                        }
733	
734	                        if !cwd_found.starts_with(cwd_prefix) { continue }
735	
736	                        sessions.push(RepoSession {
737	                            session_id,
738	                            session_name: display_name,
739	                            updated_at: file_mtime,
740	                            branch: git_branch,
741	                            agent: String::new(),
742	                            backed_up: false,
743	                        });
744	                    }
745	                }
746	            }
747	        }
748	    }
749	
750	    sessions.sort_by(|a, b| b.updated_at.cmp(&a.updated_at));
751	    sessions
752	}
753	
754	// ── Text input prompt ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
755	
756	/// Generic single-line input shown in the status bar. Returns None on Esc.
757	fn collect_text_input(stdout: &mut impl Write, prefix: &str, w: usize, h: usize) -> Option<String> {
758	    let mut value = String::new();
759	    execute!(stdout, cursor::Show).ok();
760	    draw_input_bar(stdout, prefix, &value, w, h).ok();
761	
762	    loop {
763	        match event::read().ok()? {
764	            Event::Key(k) => match k.code {
765	                KeyCode::Esc => {
766	                    execute!(stdout, cursor::Hide).ok();
767	                    return None;
768	                }
769	                KeyCode::Enter => {
770	                    execute!(stdout, cursor::Hide).ok();
771	                    return Some(value);
772	                }
773	                KeyCode::Backspace => {
774	                    value.pop();
775	                    draw_input_bar(stdout, prefix, &value, w, h).ok();
776	                }
777	                KeyCode::Char(c) => {
778	                    value.push(c);
779	                    draw_input_bar(stdout, prefix, &value, w, h).ok();
780	                }
781	                _ => {}
782	            },
783	            _ => {}
784	        }
785	    }
786	}
787	
788	fn draw_input_bar(stdout: &mut impl Write, prefix: &str, value: &str, w: usize, h: usize) -> io::Result<()> {
789	    let prefix_len = prefix.chars().count();
790	    let text: String = value.chars().take(w.saturating_sub(prefix_len + 2)).collect();
791	    let content = format!("{prefix}{text}");
792	    let padded = format!("{:<width$}", content, width = w);
793	    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (h - 1) as u16))?;
794	    write!(stdout, "\x1b[7m{padded}\x1b[0m")?;
795	    let col = (prefix_len + text.chars().count()).min(w.saturating_sub(1)) as u16;
796	    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(col, (h - 1) as u16))?;
797	    stdout.flush()
798	}
799	
800	fn collect_search_query(stdout: &mut impl Write, w: usize, h: usize) -> Option<String> {
801	    collect_text_input(stdout, "  / ", w, h)
802	}

804	// ── New session wizard ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
805	
806	/// A 4-step panel drawn over the bottom of the screen. Each completed step
807	/// stays visible with its confirmed value while the user fills the next one.
808	fn new_session_wizard(stdout: &mut impl Write, repo_dir: &str, w: usize, h: usize) -> Option<NewSessionConfig> {
809	    const LABEL_W: usize = 24;
810	    // Panel rows: title, blank, agent, branch, name, prompt, blank  (7 rows + 1 status bar)
811	    const PANEL_H: usize = 7;
812	
813	    let panel_top = h.saturating_sub(PANEL_H + 1);
814	
815	    let mut step = 0usize;           // 0=agent 1=branch 2=name 3=prompt
816	    let mut agent_sel = 0usize;
817	    let mut confirmed_agent = 0usize;
818	    let mut inputs = [String::new(), String::new(), String::new()]; // branch, name, prompt
819	
820	    execute!(stdout, cursor::Show).ok();
821	
822	    loop {
823	        // ── Draw panel ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
824	        // Title row
825	        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, panel_top as u16)).ok();
826	        write!(stdout, "\x1b[1;38;5;229m  New Session\x1b[0m").ok();
827	        execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine)).ok();
828	
829	        // Blank separator
830	        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (panel_top + 1) as u16)).ok();
831	        execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine)).ok();
832	
833	        let step_rows  = [panel_top + 2, panel_top + 3, panel_top + 4, panel_top + 5];
834	        let step_labels = ["  Agent", "  Worktree branch", "  Session name", "  Prompt"];
835	
836	        for (i, (&row, &label)) in step_rows.iter().zip(step_labels.iter()).enumerate() {
837	            execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16)).ok();
838	            let lpart = format!("{label:<LABEL_W$}");
839	
840	            if i < step {
841	                // Completed — dim label, value in colour
842	                let val = if i == 0 {
843	                    let (name, _, col) = AGENTS[confirmed_agent];
844	                    format!("\x1b[38;5;{col}m{name}\x1b[0m")
845	                } else {
846	                    let v = inputs[i - 1].trim();
847	                    if v.is_empty() { "\x1b[38;5;238m(skip)\x1b[0m".to_string() }
848	                    else            { format!("\x1b[38;5;255m{v}\x1b[0m") }
849	                };
850	                write!(stdout, "\x1b[38;5;240m{lpart}\x1b[0m{val}").ok();
851	            } else if i == step {
852	                // Active step
853	                write!(stdout, "\x1b[38;5;255m{lpart}\x1b[0m").ok();
854	                if i == 0 {
855	                    // Inline agent picker
856	                    for (ai, (name, _, col)) in AGENTS.iter().enumerate() {
857	                        if ai > 0 { write!(stdout, "\x1b[38;5;238m │ \x1b[0m").ok(); }
858	                        if ai == agent_sel {
859	                            write!(stdout, "\x1b[48;5;236;38;5;{col}m {name} \x1b[0m").ok();
860	                        } else {
861	                            write!(stdout, "\x1b[38;5;240m {name}\x1b[0m").ok();
862	                        }
863	                    }
864	                } else {
865	                    // Text input — print typed chars, terminal cursor sits here
866	                    write!(stdout, "\x1b[38;5;255m{}\x1b[0m", inputs[i - 1]).ok();
867	                }
868	            } else {
869	                // Future step — very dim label only
870	                write!(stdout, "\x1b[38;5;238m{lpart}\x1b[0m").ok();
871	            }
872	            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine)).ok();
873	        }
874	
875	        // Trailing blank row
876	        let blank2 = panel_top + 6;
877	        if blank2 < h.saturating_sub(1) {
878	            execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, blank2 as u16)).ok();
879	            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine)).ok();
880	        }
881	
882	        // Status bar hint
883	        let bar = if step == 0 {
884	            "  ↑↓/jk: select agent   enter: confirm   esc: cancel  "
885	        } else {
886	            "  type to enter   enter: next   esc: cancel  "
887	        };
888	        draw_status(stdout, bar, w, h).ok();
889	
890	        // Position terminal cursor for text input steps
891	        if step > 0 {
892	            let row = step_rows[step];
893	            let col = (LABEL_W + inputs[step - 1].len()).min(w.saturating_sub(1));
894	            execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(col as u16, row as u16)).ok();
895	        }
896	        stdout.flush().ok();
897	
898	        // ── Handle input ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
899	        match event::read().ok()? {
900	            Event::Key(k) => match k.code {
901	                KeyCode::Esc => {
902	                    execute!(stdout, cursor::Hide).ok();
903	                    return None;
904	                }
905	                KeyCode::Up   | KeyCode::Char('k') if step == 0 => {
906	                    if agent_sel > 0 { agent_sel -= 1; }
907	                }
908	                KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') if step == 0 => {
909	                    if agent_sel + 1 < AGENTS.len() { agent_sel += 1; }
910	                }
911	                KeyCode::Backspace if step > 0 => {
912	                    inputs[step - 1].pop();
913	                }
914	                KeyCode::Char(c) if step > 0 && !k.modifiers.contains(crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::CONTROL) => {
915	                    inputs[step - 1].push(c);
916	                }
917	                KeyCode::Enter => match step {
918	                    0 => { confirmed_agent = agent_sel; step = 1; }
919	                    1 | 2 => { step += 1; }
920	                    _ => {
921	                        execute!(stdout, cursor::Hide).ok();
922	                        let (agent_name, agent_cli, _) = AGENTS[confirmed_agent];
923	                        let branch_raw = inputs[0].trim().to_string();
924	                        return Some(NewSessionConfig {
925	                            agent_name: agent_name.to_string(),
926	                            agent_cli: agent_cli.to_string(),
927	                            branch: if branch_raw.is_empty() { None } else { Some(branch_raw) },
928	                            session_name: inputs[1].trim().to_string(),
929	                            prompt: inputs[2].trim().to_string(),
930	                            repo_dir: repo_dir.to_string(),
931	                        });
932	                    }
933	                },
934	                _ => {}
935	            },
936	            _ => {}
937	        }
938	    }
939	}

942	
943	fn create_worktree(repo_dir: &str, branch: &str) -> String {
944	    let repo_path = std::path::Path::new(repo_dir);
945	    let parent = match repo_path.parent() {
946	        Some(p) => p,
947	        None => return "Error: cannot determine repo parent directory".to_string(),
948	    };
949	    let repo_name = repo_path
950	        .file_name()
951	        .map(|s| s.to_string_lossy().to_string())
952	        .unwrap_or_else(|| "repo".to_string());
953	    let wt_path = parent.join(format!("{repo_name}-{branch}"));
954	
955	    // Try -b (new branch) first; fall back to checking out an existing branch.
956	    let out = std::process::Command::new("git")
957	        .args(["worktree", "add", "-b", branch, wt_path.to_str().unwrap_or("")])
958	        .current_dir(repo_dir)
959	        .output();
960	
961	    match out {
962	        Err(e) => format!("Error: {e}"),
963	        Ok(o) if o.status.success() => {
964	            format!("Created worktree: {}", short_path_s(wt_path.to_str().unwrap_or("")))
965	        }
966	        Ok(o) => {
967	            let msg = String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stderr);
968	            let first = msg.lines().find(|l| !l.trim().is_empty()).unwrap_or("unknown error");
969	            format!("Error: {first}")
970	        }
971	    }
972	}

976	fn short_path_s(path: &str) -> String {
977	    let home = std::env::var("HOME").unwrap_or_default();
978	    if !home.is_empty() && path.starts_with(&home) {
979	        format!("~{}", &path[home.len()..])
980	    } else {
981	        path.to_string()
982	    }
983	}

985	fn user_text(content: &serde_json::Value) -> Option<String> {
986	    match content {
987	        serde_json::Value::String(s) if !s.trim().is_empty() => Some(s.trim().to_string()),
988	        serde_json::Value::Array(blocks) => {
989	            blocks.iter().find_map(|b| {
990	                if b["type"].as_str() == Some("text") {
991	                    b["text"].as_str().filter(|t| !t.trim().is_empty()).map(|t| t.trim().to_string())
992	                } else { None }
993	            })
994	        }
995	        _ => None,
996	    }
997	}

999	fn with_bg(s: &str, bg: &str) -> String {
1000	    let reinsert = format!("\x1b[0m\x1b[{bg}m");
1001	    let body = s.replace("\x1b[0m", &reinsert);
1002	    format!("\x1b[{bg}m{body}")
1003	}
1004	
1005	fn visible_width(s: &str) -> usize {
1006	    let mut w = 0usize;
1007	    let mut chars = s.chars().peekable();
1008	    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
1009	        if c == '\x1b' {
1010	            for nc in chars.by_ref() { if nc.is_ascii_alphabetic() { break; } }
1011	        } else {
1012	            w += 1;
1013	        }
1014	    }
1015	    w
1016	}
1017	
1018	fn relative_time(dt: DateTime<Utc>) -> String {
1019	    let secs = (Utc::now() - dt).num_seconds().max(0);
1020	    if secs < 604_800 {
1021	        match secs {
1022	            s if s < 60     => "just now".to_string(),
1023	            s if s < 3_600  => format!("{} min{} ago", s/60,     if s/60==1     {""} else {"s"}),
1024	            s if s < 86_400 => format!("{} hr{} ago",  s/3_600,  if s/3_600==1  {""} else {"s"}),
1025	            s               => format!("{} day{} ago", s/86_400, if s/86_400==1 {""} else {"s"}),
1026	        }
1027	    } else {
1028	        dt.with_timezone(&Local).format("%m/%d/%y").to_string()
1029	    }
1030	}
1031	
1032	pub fn run_for_dir(repo_dir: &str) -> Result<()> {
1033	    let conn = db::connect()?;
1034	    let repos = fetch_repos(&conn)?;
1035	    let start = repos.iter().position(|r| r.directory == repo_dir);
1036	
1037	    let orig_hook = std::panic::take_hook();
1038	    std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |info| {
1039	        let mut out = io::stdout();
1040	        let _ = execute!(out, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show);
1041	        let _ = terminal::disable_raw_mode();
1042	        orig_hook(info);
1043	    }));
1044	
1045	    let mut stdout = io::stdout();
1046	    terminal::enable_raw_mode()?;
1047	    execute!(stdout, EnterAlternateScreen, cursor::Hide)?;
1048	
1049	    let outcome = tui_loop(&mut stdout, &repos, false, start);
1050	
1051	    execute!(stdout, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show)?;
1052	    terminal::disable_raw_mode()?;
1053	
1054	    match outcome? {
1055	        Some(TuiOutcome::ResumeSession { id, cwd }) => {
1056	            super::show::resume_session(&id, &cwd);
1057	        }
1058	        Some(TuiOutcome::LaunchAgent { cli, cwd, session_name, prompt }) => {
1059	            use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
1060	            let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(&cli);
1061	            if !cwd.is_empty() { cmd.current_dir(&cwd); }
1062	            if !session_name.is_empty() { cmd.args(["-n", &session_name]); }
1063	            if !prompt.is_empty() { cmd.arg(&prompt); }
1064	            let err = cmd.exec();
1065	            eprintln!("Failed to launch {cli}: {err}");
1066	        }
1067	        _ => {}
1068	    }
1069	
1070	    Ok(())
1071	}
1072	
1073	pub fn fetch_repos(conn: &rusqlite::Connection) -> Result<Vec<Repository>> {
1074	    let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT id, directory, remote, name FROM repositories ORDER BY name")?;
1075	    let repos = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
1076	        Ok(Repository {
1077	            id: row.get(0)?,
1078	            directory: row.get(1)?,
1079	            remote: row.get(2)?,
1080	            name: row.get(3)?,
1081	        })
1082	    })?
1083	    .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
1084	    Ok(repos)
1085	}
```

```
31	pub fn run(all: bool, json: bool) -> Result<()> {
32	    let conn = db::connect()?;
33	
34	    let repos = fetch_repos(&conn)?;
35	    let cwd_env = env::current_dir().ok().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());
36	    let current_repo_dir: Option<String> = cwd_env.as_deref().and_then(|cwd| {
37	        repos.iter()
38	            .find(|r| cwd.starts_with(r.directory.as_str()))
39	            .map(|r| r.directory.clone())
40	    });
41	
42	    let mut stmt = conn.prepare(
43	        "SELECT session_id, agent_name, user, created_at, updated_at, cwd, session_name, tokens_used
44	         FROM sessions ORDER BY updated_at DESC"
45	    )?;
46	
47	    let db_sessions: Vec<Session> = stmt.query_map([], |row| {
48	        let created_str: String = row.get(3)?;
49	        let updated_str: String = row.get(4)?;
50	        Ok(Session {
51	            session_id: row.get(0)?,
52	            agent_name: row.get(1)?,
53	            user: row.get(2)?,
54	            created_at: DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&created_str)
55	                .map(|dt| dt.with_timezone(&Utc))
56	                .unwrap_or_else(|_| Utc::now()),
57	            updated_at: DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&updated_str)
58	                .map(|dt| dt.with_timezone(&Utc))
59	                .unwrap_or_else(|_| Utc::now()),
60	            cwd: row.get(5)?,
61	            session_name: row.get(6)?,
62	            tokens_used: row.get(7)?,
63	        })
64	    })?
65	    .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
66	
67	    let known_ids: HashSet<String> = db_sessions.iter().map(|s| s.session_id.clone()).collect();
68	    let cutoff = Utc::now() - chrono::Duration::days(3);
69	
70	    let mut sessions: Vec<DisplaySession> = db_sessions.into_iter()
71	        .filter(|s| all || s.updated_at >= cutoff)
72	        .map(|s| DisplaySession {
73	            session_id: s.session_id,
74	            session_name: s.session_name,

79	            backed_up: true,

81	        .collect();
82	
83	    sessions.extend(scan_claude_projects(&known_ids, all, cutoff));
84	
85	    // Local-repo sessions first, then by recency
86	    sessions.sort_by(|a, b| {
87	        let a_local = current_repo_dir.as_deref().map_or(false, |d| a.cwd.starts_with(d));
88	        let b_local = current_repo_dir.as_deref().map_or(false, |d| b.cwd.starts_with(d));
89	        b_local.cmp(&a_local).then(b.updated_at.cmp(&a.updated_at))
90	    });

136	
137	fn tui_loop(stdout: &mut impl Write, sessions: &[DisplaySession]) -> Option<Action> {
138	    let mut sel = 0usize;
139	
140	    loop {
141	        let (w, h) = terminal::size().unwrap_or((120, 40));
142	        let w = w as usize;
143	        let h = h as usize;
144	
145	        draw(stdout, sessions, sel, w, h).ok();
146	
147	        match event::read().ok()? {
148	            Event::Key(k) => match k.code {
149	                KeyCode::Char('q') | KeyCode::Esc => break,
150	                KeyCode::Char('c') if k.modifiers.contains(crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::CONTROL) => {
151	                    execute!(stdout, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show).ok();
152	                    terminal::disable_raw_mode().ok();
153	                    std::process::exit(0);
154	                }
155	                KeyCode::Up   | KeyCode::Char('k') => { if sel > 0 { sel -= 1; } }
156	                KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => { if sel + 1 < sessions.len() { sel += 1; } }
157	                KeyCode::Char('g') => { sel = 0; }
158	                KeyCode::Char('G') => { sel = sessions.len().saturating_sub(1); }
159	                KeyCode::Char(' ') | KeyCode::Right | KeyCode::Enter => {
160	                    return Some(Action::Show(sessions[sel].session_id.clone()));
161	                }
162	                KeyCode::Char('r') => {
163	                    let s = &sessions[sel];
164	                    return Some(Action::Resume(s.session_id.clone(), s.cwd.clone()));
165	                }
166	                _ => {}
167	            },
168	            Event::Resize(_, _) => {
169	                execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::All)).ok();
170	            }
171	            _ => {}
172	        }
173	    }
174	
175	    None
176	}
177	
178	fn draw(stdout: &mut impl Write, sessions: &[DisplaySession], sel: usize, w: usize, h: usize) -> io::Result<()> {
179	    const SEL_BG: &str = "48;5;236";
180	
181	    let content_h = h.saturating_sub(1);
182	    let scroll = if sel >= content_h { sel + 1 - content_h } else { 0 };
183	
184	    // Pre-compute column widths
185	    let name_w = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.session_name.trim().chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0).min(40);
186	    let cwd_w  = sessions.iter().map(|s| short_cwd(&s.cwd).chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);
187	    let branch_w = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.branch.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);
188	    let agent_w  = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.agent_name.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);
189	
190	    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, 0))?;
191	
192	    for row in 0..content_h {
193	        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16))?;
194	        let idx = scroll + row;
195	        if idx >= sessions.len() {
196	            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
197	            continue;
198	        }
199	
200	        let s = &sessions[idx];
201	        let is_sel = idx == sel;
202	
203	        let id_short: String = s.session_id.chars().take(8).collect();
204	        let ts = relative_time(s.updated_at);
205	        let age = (Utc::now() - s.updated_at).num_seconds().max(0);
206	        let dot_col = match age {
207	            a if a < 900   => "38;5;82",
208	            a if a < 3_600 => "38;5;214",
209	            _              => "38;5;240",
210	        };
211	        let (name_col, meta_col, dot_char) = if s.backed_up {
212	            ("1;38;5;255", "38;5;240", "*")
213	        } else {
214	            ("38;5;242", "38;5;238", "·")
215	        };
216	        let branch_col = if s.backed_up { "38;5;75" } else { "38;5;239" };
217	
218	        let name: String = s.session_name.trim().chars().take(name_w).collect();
219	        let name_padded = format!("{:<name_w$}", name);
220	
221	        let cwd_short = short_cwd(&s.cwd);
222	        let cwd_padded = format!("{:<cwd_w$}", cwd_short);
223	
224	        let mut line = format!(
225	            "\x1b[{dot_col}m{dot_char}\x1b[0m \x1b[{name_col}m{name_padded}\x1b[0m  \x1b[38;5;240m{cwd_padded}\x1b[0m"
226	        );
227	
228	        if branch_w > 0 {
229	            let b: String = s.branch.chars().take(branch_w).collect();
230	            let pad = " ".repeat(branch_w - b.chars().count());
231	            line.push_str(&format!("  \x1b[{branch_col}m{b}{pad}\x1b[0m"));
232	        }
233	
234	        if agent_w > 0 {
235	            let col = if s.backed_up { agent_color(&s.agent_name) } else { 239 };
236	            let a: String = s.agent_name.chars().take(agent_w).collect();
237	            let pad = " ".repeat(agent_w - a.chars().count());
238	            line.push_str(&format!("  \x1b[38;5;{col}m{a}{pad}\x1b[0m"));
239	        }
240	
241	        line.push_str(&format!("  \x1b[{meta_col}m{id_short}  {ts}\x1b[0m"));
242	
243	        if is_sel {
244	            let colored = with_bg(&line, SEL_BG);
245	            let vis = visible_width(&line);
246	            let pad = w.saturating_sub(vis);
247	            write!(stdout, "\x1b[{SEL_BG}m{colored}{}\x1b[0m", " ".repeat(pad))?;
248	        } else {
249	            write!(stdout, "{line}")?;
250	            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
251	        }
252	    }
253	
254	    let bar = format!(
255	        "  {} sessions   ↑↓/jk navigate   space/enter: view   r: resume   q: quit  ",
256	        sessions.len()
257	    );
258	    let display: String = bar.chars().take(w).collect();
259	    let padded = format!("{:<width$}", display, width = w);
260	    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (h - 1) as u16))?;
261	    write!(stdout, "\x1b[7m{padded}\x1b[0m")?;
262	
263	    stdout.flush()
264	}
265	
266	fn scan_claude_projects(known_ids: &HashSet<String>, all: bool, cutoff: DateTime<Utc>) -> Vec<DisplaySession> {
267	    let home = match std::env::var("HOME") {
268	        Ok(h) => h,
269	        Err(_) => return vec![],
270	    };
271	    let projects_dir = PathBuf::from(&home).join(".claude/projects");
272	    let Ok(projects) = std::fs::read_dir(&projects_dir) else { return vec![] };
273	
274	    let mut sessions: Vec<DisplaySession> = Vec::new();
275	
276	    for project_entry in projects.flatten() {
277	        let project_dir = project_entry.path();
278	        if !project_dir.is_dir() { continue }
279	
280	        let Ok(files) = std::fs::read_dir(&project_dir) else { continue };
281	
282	        for file_entry in files.flatten() {
283	            let path = file_entry.path();
284	            if path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) != Some("jsonl") { continue }
285	
286	            let session_id = match path.file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) {
287	                Some(s) => s.to_string(),
288	                None => continue,
289	            };
290	
291	            let file_mtime = file_entry.metadata().ok()
292	                .and_then(|m| m.modified().ok())
293	                .map(DateTime::<Utc>::from)
294	                .unwrap_or_else(Utc::now);
295	
296	            if !all && file_mtime < cutoff { continue }
297	
298	            let Ok(file) = std::fs::File::open(&path) else { continue };
299	            let reader = std::io::BufReader::new(file);
300	            let mut session_name = String::new();
301	            let mut cwd = String::new();
302	            let mut branch = String::new();
303	            let mut last_prompt = String::new();
304	
305	            for line in reader.lines().flatten() {
306	                if line.trim().is_empty() { continue }
307	                let Ok(v) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&line) else { continue };
308	                match v["type"].as_str() {
309	                    Some("custom-title") => {
310	                        if let Some(t) = v["customTitle"].as_str() { session_name = t.to_string(); }
311	                    }
312	                    Some("user") | Some("system") => {
313	                        if cwd.is_empty() {
314	                            if let Some(c) = v["cwd"].as_str() { cwd = c.to_string(); }
315	                        }
316	                    }
317	                    _ => {}
318	                }
319	                if v["type"].as_str() == Some("user") {
320	                    if let Some(t) = user_text(&v["message"]["content"]) {
321	                        last_prompt = t;
322	                    }
323	                }
324	                if let Some(b) = v["gitBranch"].as_str() {
325	                    if !b.is_empty() { branch = b.to_string(); }
326	                }
327	            }
328	
329	            let display_name = if !session_name.is_empty() { session_name } else { last_prompt };
330	
331	            if known_ids.contains(&session_id) {
332	                if let Some(existing) = sessions.iter_mut().find(|s| s.session_id == session_id) {
333	                    if !display_name.is_empty() { existing.session_name = display_name; }
334	                    if file_mtime > existing.updated_at { existing.updated_at = file_mtime; }
335	                    if !branch.is_empty() { existing.branch = branch; }
336	                }
337	                continue;
338	            }
339	
340	            sessions.push(DisplaySession {
341	                session_id,
342	                session_name: display_name,
343	                cwd,
344	                branch,
345	                updated_at: file_mtime,
346	                agent_name: "Claude Code".to_string(),
347	                backed_up: false,
348	            });
349	        }
350	    }
351	
352	    sessions
353	}
354	
355	fn user_text(content: &serde_json::Value) -> Option<String> {
356	    match content {
357	        serde_json::Value::String(s) if !s.trim().is_empty() => Some(s.trim().to_string()),
358	        serde_json::Value::Array(blocks) => {
359	            blocks.iter().find_map(|b| {
360	                if b["type"].as_str() == Some("text") {
361	                    b["text"].as_str().filter(|t| !t.trim().is_empty()).map(|t| t.trim().to_string())
362	                } else { None }
363	            })
364	        }
365	        _ => None,
366	    }
367	}
368	
369	use super::agent_color;
370	
371	fn relative_time(dt: DateTime<Utc>) -> String {
372	    let secs = (Utc::now() - dt).num_seconds().max(0);
373	    if secs < 604_800 {
374	        match secs {
375	            s if s < 60     => "just now".to_string(),
376	            s if s < 3_600  => format!("{} min{} ago", s/60,     if s/60==1     {""} else {"s"}),
377	            s if s < 86_400 => format!("{} hr{} ago",  s/3_600,  if s/3_600==1  {""} else {"s"}),
378	            s               => format!("{} day{} ago", s/86_400, if s/86_400==1 {""} else {"s"}),
379	        }
380	    } else {
381	        dt.with_timezone(&Local).format("%m/%d/%y").to_string()
382	    }
383	}
384	
385	fn short_cwd(cwd: &str) -> String {
386	    let home = std::env::var("HOME").unwrap_or_default();
387	    let path = if !home.is_empty() && cwd.starts_with(&home) {
388	        format!("~{}", &cwd[home.len()..])
389	    } else {
390	        cwd.to_string()
391	    };
392	    let parts: Vec<&str> = path.trim_end_matches('/').split('/').filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).collect();
393	    match parts.len() {
394	        0 => "/".to_string(),
395	        1 => format!("/{}", parts[0]),
396	        2 => format!("{}/{}", parts[0], parts[1]),
397	        _ => format!("…/{}/{}", parts[parts.len() - 2], parts[parts.len() - 1]),
398	    }
399	}
400	
401	fn with_bg(s: &str, bg: &str) -> String {
402	    let reinsert = format!("\x1b[0m\x1b[{bg}m");
403	    let body = s.replace("\x1b[0m", &reinsert);
404	    format!("\x1b[{bg}m{body}")
405	}
406	
407	fn visible_width(s: &str) -> usize {
408	    let mut w = 0usize;
409	    let mut chars = s.chars().peekable();
410	    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
411	        if c == '\x1b' {
412	            for nc in chars.by_ref() { if nc.is_ascii_alphabetic() { break; } }
413	        } else {
414	            w += 1;
415	        }
416	    }
417	    w
418	}
419
```

```
49	pub fn run_at(session_id: &str, start_ts: Option<&str>) -> Result<()> {
50	    let path = find_session(session_id)
51	        .with_context(|| format!("no session file found for '{session_id}'"))?;
52	
53	    let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)
54	        .with_context(|| format!("cannot read {}", path.display()))?;
55	
56	    // Look up agent name and repo info from the gossamer DB using the session UUID.
57	    let uuid = path.file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("");
58	    let agent = if let Ok(conn) = crate::db::connect() {
59	        conn.query_row(
60	            "SELECT agent_name FROM sessions WHERE session_id = ?1",
61	            [uuid],
62	            |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
63	        ).unwrap_or_default()
64	    } else { String::new() };
65	
66	    let mut cards = parse(&raw, &agent);
67	    if cards.is_empty() {
68	        println!("No messages found.");
69	        return Ok(());
70	    }
71	
72	    // Extract branch and cwd from the Header before cards are consumed by pager.
73	    let session_branch = cards.iter().find_map(|c| {
74	        if let Card::Header { branch, .. } = c { Some(branch.clone()) } else { None }
75	    }).unwrap_or_default();
76	    let session_cwd = cards.iter().find_map(|c| {
77	        if let Card::Header { cwd, .. } = c { Some(cwd.clone()) } else { None }
78	    }).unwrap_or_default();
79	
80	    // Look up the repo that owns this session's cwd and prepend a RepoLink card.
81	    if let Ok(conn) = crate::db::connect() {
82	        if let Ok((repo_name, repo_dir)) = conn.query_row(
83	            "SELECT name, directory FROM repositories
84	             WHERE ?1 LIKE (directory || '%')
85	             ORDER BY LENGTH(directory) DESC LIMIT 1",
86	            [&session_cwd],
87	            |row| Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, String>(1)?)),
88	        ) {
89	            cards.insert(0, Card::RepoLink { name: repo_name, dir: repo_dir, branch: session_branch.clone() });
90	        }
91	    }
92	
93	    loop {
94	        match pager(&cards, start_ts)? {
95	            PagerOutcome::Resume => {
96	                do_resume(&agent, session_id, &session_branch, &session_cwd);
97	                break;
98	            }
99	            PagerOutcome::Delete => { super::clean::run(session_id, false)?; break; }
100	            PagerOutcome::Quit   => break,
```
