```
1	use anyhow::Result;
2	use chrono::{DateTime, Duration, Utc};
3	use crossterm::{
4	    cursor,
5	    event::{self, Event, KeyCode},
6	    execute,
7	    terminal::{self, ClearType},
8	};
9	use std::io::Write;
10	use std::path::PathBuf;
11	
12	use crate::{db, entity::repository::Repository};
13	
14	pub struct StaleWorktree {
15	    pub repo_dir: String,
16	    pub path: String,
17	    pub branch: String,
18	}
19	
20	// ── Core logic ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
21	
22	fn worktree_paths(repo_dir: &str) -> Vec<(String, String, bool)> {
23	    let out = std::process::Command::new("git")
24	        .args(["worktree", "list", "--porcelain"])
25	        .current_dir(repo_dir)
26	        .output();
27	
28	    let Ok(out) = out else { return vec![]; };
29	    if !out.status.success() { return vec![]; }
30	    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
31	
32	    let mut result = Vec::new();
33	    let mut first = true;
34	
35	    for block in text.split("\n\n") {
36	        let mut path = String::new();
37	        let mut branch = String::new();
38	        let mut detached = false;
39	
40	        for line in block.lines() {
41	            if let Some(v) = line.strip_prefix("worktree ") { path = v.to_string(); }
42	            else if let Some(v) = line.strip_prefix("branch refs/heads/") { branch = v.to_string(); }
43	            else if line == "detached" { detached = true; }
44	        }
45	
46	        if path.is_empty() { continue; }
47	        if detached { branch = "(detached)".to_string(); }
48	        if branch.is_empty() { continue; }
49	
50	        let is_main = first;
51	        result.push((path, branch, is_main));
52	        first = false;
53	    }
54	
55	    result
56	}
57	
58	fn is_worktree_active(wt_path: &str, threshold: DateTime<Utc>) -> bool {
59	    // Check gossamer DB for sessions whose cwd is inside this worktree
60	    if let Ok(conn) = db::connect() {
61	        let pattern = format!("{wt_path}%");
62	        let threshold_str = threshold.to_rfc3339();
63	        let count: i64 = conn.query_row(
64	            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sessions WHERE cwd LIKE ?1 AND updated_at >= ?2",
65	            rusqlite::params![pattern, threshold_str],
66	            |row| row.get(0),
67	        ).unwrap_or(0);
68	        if count > 0 { return true; }
69	    }
70	
71	    // Check Claude JSONL files for recently-modified sessions whose cwd matches
72	    let Ok(home) = std::env::var("HOME") else { return false };
73	    let projects = PathBuf::from(&home).join(".claude/projects");
74	    let Ok(dirs) = std::fs::read_dir(&projects) else { return false };
75	
76	    for dir_entry in dirs.flatten() {
77	        let dir = dir_entry.path();
78	        if !dir.is_dir() { continue; }
79	        let Ok(files) = std::fs::read_dir(&dir) else { continue };
80	        for f in files.flatten() {
81	            let path = f.path();
82	            if path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) != Some("jsonl") { continue; }
83	
84	            let mtime: DateTime<Utc> = f.metadata().ok()
85	                .and_then(|m| m.modified().ok())
86	                .map(DateTime::from)
87	                .unwrap_or_else(Utc::now);
88	            if mtime < threshold { continue; }
89	
90	            let Ok(content) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path) else { continue };
91	            for line in content.lines() {
92	                let Ok(v) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(line) else { continue };
93	                if v["cwd"].as_str().is_some_and(|c| c.starts_with(wt_path)) {
94	                    return true;
95	                }
96	            }
97	        }
98	    }
99	
100	    false
101	}
102	
103	pub fn find_stale(repos: &[Repository], age_days: i64) -> Vec<StaleWorktree> {
104	    let threshold = Utc::now() - Duration::days(age_days);
105	    let mut stale = Vec::new();
106	
107	    for repo in repos {
108	        for (path, branch, is_main) in worktree_paths(&repo.directory) {
109	            if is_main { continue; }
110	            if !is_worktree_active(&path, threshold) {
111	                stale.push(StaleWorktree {
112	                    repo_dir: repo.directory.clone(),
113	                    path,
114	                    branch,
115	                });
116	            }
117	        }
118	    }
119	
120	    stale
121	}
122	
123	fn remove_worktree(repo_dir: &str, wt_path: &str, force: bool) -> Result<()> {
124	    let mut args = vec!["worktree", "remove", wt_path];
125	    if force { args.push("--force"); }
126	    let out = std::process::Command::new("git")
127	        .args(&args)
128	        .current_dir(repo_dir)
129	        .output()?;
130	    if !out.status.success() {
131	        let msg = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
132	        anyhow::bail!("{}", msg.trim());
133	    }
134	    Ok(())
135	}
136	
137	fn short_path(path: &str) -> String {
138	    let home = std::env::var("HOME").unwrap_or_default();
139	    if !home.is_empty() && path.starts_with(&home) {
140	        format!("~{}", &path[home.len()..])
141	    } else {
142	        path.to_string()
143	    }
144	}
145	
146	// ── Session cleanup ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
147	
148	/// Delete all sessions (DB rows, event log, search index) whose cwd was inside `wt_path`.
149	/// Returns the session IDs that were removed.
150	fn clean_sessions_for_worktree(wt_path: &str) -> Vec<String> {
151	    let Ok(conn) = db::connect() else { return vec![]; };
152	    let pattern = format!("{wt_path}%");
153	
154	    let mut stmt = match conn.prepare("SELECT session_id FROM sessions WHERE cwd LIKE ?1") {
155	        Ok(s) => s,
156	        Err(_) => return vec![],
157	    };
158	    let ids: Vec<String> = stmt
159	        .query_map([&pattern], |row| row.get(0))
160	        .map(|rows| rows.flatten().collect())
161	        .unwrap_or_default();
162	
163	    let mut removed = Vec::new();
164	    for id in &ids {
165	        let _ = std::process::Command::new("entire").arg("clean").arg(id).status();
166	        let _ = conn.execute("DELETE FROM event_log WHERE session_id = ?1", rusqlite::params![id]);
167	        if conn.execute("DELETE FROM sessions WHERE session_id = ?1", rusqlite::params![id]).is_ok() {
168	            removed.push(id.clone());
169	        }
170	        if let Ok(mut wc_db) = crate::ingest::open_search_db() {
171	            use witchcraft::types::{
172	                SqlConditionInternal, SqlOperator, SqlStatementInternal, SqlStatementType, SqlValue,
173	            };
174	            let filter = SqlStatementInternal {
175	                statement_type: SqlStatementType::Condition,
176	                condition: Some(SqlConditionInternal {
177	                    key: "$.session_id".to_string(),
178	                    operator: SqlOperator::Equals,
179	                    value: Some(SqlValue::String(id.clone())),
180	                }),
181	                logic: None,
182	                statements: None,
183	            };
184	            let _ = wc_db.delete_with_filter(&filter);
185	        }
186	    }
187	    removed
188	}
189	
190	// ── CLI entry point ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
191	
192	pub fn run(age_days: i64, dry_run: bool, force: bool, clean_sessions: bool, json: bool) -> Result<()> {
193	    let conn = db::connect()?;
194	    let repos = super::status::fetch_repos(&conn)?;
195	    let stale = find_stale(&repos, age_days);
196	
197	    if stale.is_empty() {
198	        if json {
199	            println!("{}", serde_json::json!({"removed": [], "dry_run": dry_run}));
200	        } else {
201	            println!("No stale worktrees found (threshold: {} days).", age_days);
202	        }
203	        return Ok(());
204	    }
205	
206	    if dry_run {
207	        if !json {
208	            println!("Stale worktrees (would be removed):");
209	            for wt in &stale {
210	                println!("  {} [{}]", short_path(&wt.path), wt.branch);
211	            }
212	        } else {
213	            let paths: Vec<_> = stale.iter().map(|w| serde_json::json!({
214	                "path": short_path(&w.path), "branch": w.branch,
215	            })).collect();
216	            println!("{}", serde_json::json!({"would_remove": paths, "dry_run": true}));
217	        }
218	        return Ok(());
219	    }
220	
221	    if !json {
222	        println!("Removing stale worktrees:");
223	    }
224	
225	    let mut removed = Vec::new();
226	    let mut errors: Vec<(String, String)> = Vec::new();
227	
228	    for wt in &stale {
229	        match remove_worktree(&wt.repo_dir, &wt.path, force) {
230	            Ok(()) => {
231	                if !json { println!("  removed worktree: {}", short_path(&wt.path)); }
232	                if clean_sessions {
233	                    let ids = clean_sessions_for_worktree(&wt.path);
234	                    if !json && !ids.is_empty() {
235	                        println!("  removed {} session{}", ids.len(), if ids.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" });
236	                    }
237	                }
238	                removed.push(wt.path.clone());
239	            }
240	            Err(e) => {
241	                if !json { eprintln!("  error: {} — {e}", short_path(&wt.path)); }
242	                errors.push((wt.path.clone(), e.to_string()));
243	            }
244	        }
245	    }
246	
247	    if json {
248	        println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&serde_json::json!({
249	            "removed": removed.iter().map(|p| serde_json::json!({
250	                "path": short_path(p),
251	            })).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
252	            "errors": errors.iter().map(|(p, e)| serde_json::json!({
253	                "path": short_path(p), "error": e,
254	            })).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
255	            "dry_run": false,
256	        }))?);
257	    }
258	
259	    Ok(())
260	}
261	
262	// ── TUI entry point ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
263	
264	/// Shows stale worktrees for `repos` and asks for confirmation before removing.
265	/// Returns true if any worktrees were removed (caller should refresh the worktree list).
266	pub fn tui_tidy(
267	    stdout: &mut impl Write,
268	    repos: &[Repository],
269	    age_days: i64,
270	    clean_sessions: bool,
271	    w: usize,
272	    h: usize,
273	) -> bool {
274	    let stale = find_stale(repos, age_days);
275	    let mut clean_sessions = clean_sessions;
276	
277	    if stale.is_empty() {
278	        let msg = format!("{:<width$}", "  No stale worktrees to remove.", width = w);
279	        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (h - 1) as u16)).ok();
280	        write!(stdout, "\x1b[7m{msg}\x1b[0m").ok();
281	        stdout.flush().ok();
282	        let _ = event::read();
283	        return false;
284	    }
285	
286	    let max_items = h.saturating_sub(5);
287	    let list_h = stale.len().min(max_items).max(1);
288	    let panel_h = list_h + 2; // title + list rows
289	    let panel_top = h.saturating_sub(panel_h + 1);
290	
291	    loop {
292	        // Title
293	        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, panel_top as u16)).ok();
294	        write!(stdout, "\x1b[1;38;5;203m  Tidy — stale worktrees\x1b[0m").ok();
295	        execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine)).ok();
296	
297	        // List
298	        for (i, wt) in stale.iter().take(list_h).enumerate() {
299	            execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (panel_top + 1 + i) as u16)).ok();
300	            write!(
301	                stdout,
302	                "  \x1b[38;5;240m[{}]\x1b[0m \x1b[38;5;255m{}\x1b[0m",
303	                wt.branch,
304	                short_path(&wt.path),
305	            ).ok();
306	            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine)).ok();
307	        }
308	        if stale.len() > list_h {
309	            let overflow_row = panel_top + 1 + list_h;
310	            if overflow_row < h.saturating_sub(1) {
311	                execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, overflow_row as u16)).ok();
312	                write!(stdout, "  \x1b[38;5;240m... and {} more\x1b[0m", stale.len() - list_h).ok();
313	                execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine)).ok();
314	            }
315	        }
316	
317	        // Status bar
318	        let count = stale.len();
319	        let sess_state = if clean_sessions { "[on]" } else { "[off]" };
320	        let hint = format!(
321	            "  y: remove {count} worktree{}   s: +sessions {sess_state}   esc/n: cancel  ",
322	            if count == 1 { "" } else { "s" },
323	        );
324	        let bar = format!("{:<width$}", hint, width = w);
325	        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (h - 1) as u16)).ok();
326	        write!(stdout, "\x1b[7m{bar}\x1b[0m").ok();
327	        stdout.flush().ok();
328	
329	        match event::read().ok() {
330	            Some(Event::Key(k)) => match k.code {
331	                KeyCode::Char('y') | KeyCode::Char('Y') => {
332	                    let mut n_wt = 0usize;
333	                    let mut n_sess = 0usize;
334	                    for wt in &stale {
335	                        if remove_worktree(&wt.repo_dir, &wt.path, false).is_ok() {
336	                            n_wt += 1;
337	                            if clean_sessions {
338	                                n_sess += clean_sessions_for_worktree(&wt.path).len();
339	                            }
340	                        }
341	                    }
342	                    let mut summary = format!(
343	                        "  Removed {n_wt} worktree{}",
344	                        if n_wt == 1 { "" } else { "s" },
345	                    );
346	                    if clean_sessions {
347	                        summary.push_str(&format!(
348	                            " and {n_sess} session{}",
349	                            if n_sess == 1 { "" } else { "s" },
350	                        ));
351	                    }
352	                    summary.push('.');
353	                    let done = format!("{:<width$}", summary, width = w);
354	                    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (h - 1) as u16)).ok();
355	                    write!(stdout, "\x1b[7m{done}\x1b[0m").ok();
356	                    stdout.flush().ok();
357	                    let _ = event::read();
358	                    return n_wt > 0;
359	                }
360	                KeyCode::Char('s') | KeyCode::Char('S') => { clean_sessions = !clean_sessions; }
361	                KeyCode::Char('n') | KeyCode::Esc | KeyCode::Char('q') => return false,
362	                _ => {}
363	            },
364	            _ => {}
365	        }
366	    }
367	}
368
```

```
114	fn find_session(id: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
115	    let p = PathBuf::from(id);
116	    if p.exists() { return Some(p); }
117	    let home = std::env::var("HOME").ok()?;
118	    let projects = PathBuf::from(&home).join(".claude/projects");
119	    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&projects).ok()? {
120	        let dir = entry.ok()?.path();
121	        let candidate = dir.join(format!("{id}.jsonl"));
122	        if candidate.exists() { return Some(candidate); }
123	    }
124	    None
125	}

127	// ── Parsing ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
128	
129	fn parse(raw: &str, agent: &str) -> Vec<Card> {
130	    let mut tool_names: HashMap<String, String> = HashMap::new();
131	    for line in raw.lines() {
132	        if let Ok(v) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(line) {
133	            if let Some(blocks) = v["message"]["content"].as_array() {
134	                for b in blocks {
135	                    if b["type"].as_str() == Some("tool_use") {
136	                        if let (Some(id), Some(name)) = (b["id"].as_str(), b["name"].as_str()) {
137	                            tool_names.insert(id.to_string(), name.to_string());
138	                        }
139	                    }
140	                }
141	            }
142	        }
143	    }
144	
145	    let mut cards: Vec<Card> = Vec::new();
146	    let mut first_cwd    = String::new();
147	    let mut first_branch = String::new();
148	    let mut first_ts     = String::new();
149	    let mut title: Option<String> = None;
150	
151	    for line in raw.lines() {
152	        if line.trim().is_empty() { continue; }
153	        let Ok(v) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(line) else { continue };
154	        let ts = v["timestamp"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
155	        if first_ts.is_empty() && !ts.is_empty() { first_ts = ts.clone(); }
156	
157	        match v["type"].as_str() {
158	            Some("custom-title") => {
159	                title = v["customTitle"].as_str().map(str::to_string);
160	            }
161	            Some("system") => {
162	                if let Some(c) = v["cwd"].as_str() { if first_cwd.is_empty() { first_cwd = c.to_string(); } }
163	                let content = v["content"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
164	                if !content.is_empty() {
165	                    let subtype = v["subtype"].as_str().unwrap_or("system").replace('_', " ");
166	                    cards.push(Card::System { ts, subtype, content });
167	                }
168	            }
169	            Some("user") => {
170	                if let Some(c) = v["cwd"].as_str()       { if first_cwd.is_empty()    { first_cwd    = c.to_string(); } }
171	                if let Some(b) = v["gitBranch"].as_str() { if first_branch.is_empty() { first_branch = b.to_string(); } }
172	                let parts = parse_user(&v["message"]["content"], &tool_names);
173	                if !parts.is_empty() { cards.push(Card::UserMsg { ts, parts }); }
174	            }
175	            Some("assistant") => {
176	                let parts = parse_asst(&v["message"]["content"]);
177	                if !parts.is_empty() { cards.push(Card::AsstMsg { ts, parts }); }
178	            }
179	            _ => {}
180	        }
181	    }
182	
183	    let mut result = vec![Card::Header { title, cwd: first_cwd, branch: first_branch, ts: first_ts, agent: agent.to_string() }];
184	    result.extend(cards);
185	
186	    // Merge each pure tool-result UserMsg into the preceding AsstMsg.
187	    let mut i = 1;
188	    while i < result.len() {
189	        let is_pure_results = if let Card::UserMsg { parts, .. } = &result[i] {
190	            parts.iter().all(|p| matches!(p, UserPart::ToolResult { .. }))
191	        } else { false };
192	
193	        if is_pure_results && matches!(&result[i - 1], Card::AsstMsg { .. }) {
194	            let tool_results: Vec<(String, String, bool)> =
195	                if let Card::UserMsg { parts, .. } = &result[i] {
196	                    parts.iter().filter_map(|p| {
197	                        if let UserPart::ToolResult { id, content, is_error, .. } = p {
198	                            Some((id.clone(), content.clone(), *is_error))
199	                        } else { None }
200	                    }).collect()
201	                } else { vec![] };
202	
203	            if let Card::AsstMsg { parts: asst_parts, .. } = &mut result[i - 1] {
204	                for part in asst_parts.iter_mut() {
205	                    if let AsstPart::ToolCall { id, result: res, .. } = part {
206	                        if let Some((_, content, is_error)) =
207	                            tool_results.iter().find(|(rid, _, _)| rid == id)
208	                        {
209	                            *res = Some((content.clone(), *is_error));
210	                        }
211	                    }
212	                }
213	            }
214	            result.remove(i);
215	        } else {
216	            i += 1;
217	        }
218	    }
219	
220	    // Merge consecutive AsstMsg cards so the assistant name appears only once per run.
221	    let mut i = 1;
222	    while i < result.len() {
223	        if matches!(&result[i - 1], Card::AsstMsg { .. }) && matches!(&result[i], Card::AsstMsg { .. }) {
224	            if let Card::AsstMsg { parts: new_parts, .. } = result.remove(i) {
225	                if let Card::AsstMsg { parts, .. } = &mut result[i - 1] {
226	                    parts.extend(new_parts);
227	                }
228	            }
229	        } else {
230	            i += 1;
231	        }
232	    }
233	
234	    // Split each AsstMsg into a text-only AsstMsg (if any text) + a ToolRound (if any tools).
235	    let mut split: Vec<Card> = Vec::with_capacity(result.len());
236	    for card in result {
237	        match card {
238	            Card::AsstMsg { ts, parts } => {
239	                let (text_parts, tool_parts): (Vec<AsstPart>, Vec<AsstPart>) =
240	                    parts.into_iter().partition(|p| matches!(p, AsstPart::Text(_)));
241	                if !text_parts.is_empty() { split.push(Card::AsstMsg  { ts, parts: text_parts }); }
242	                if !tool_parts.is_empty() { split.push(Card::ToolRound { parts: tool_parts }); }
243	            }
244	            other => split.push(other),
245	        }
246	    }
247	    let mut result = split;
248	
249	    // Merge adjacent ToolRound cards (can arise when consecutive asst turns were all-tools).
250	    let mut i = 1;
251	    while i < result.len() {
252	        if matches!(&result[i - 1], Card::ToolRound { .. }) && matches!(&result[i], Card::ToolRound { .. }) {
253	            if let Card::ToolRound { parts: new_parts } = result.remove(i) {
254	                if let Card::ToolRound { parts } = &mut result[i - 1] {
255	                    parts.extend(new_parts);
256	                }
257	            }
258	        } else {
259	            i += 1;
260	        }
261	    }
262	
263	    result
264	}
265	
266	fn parse_user(content: &Value, tool_names: &HashMap<String, String>) -> Vec<UserPart> {
267	    let mut out = Vec::new();
268	    match content {
269	        Value::String(s) if !s.trim().is_empty() => out.push(UserPart::Text(s.clone())),
270	        Value::Array(blocks) => {
271	            for b in blocks {
272	                match b["type"].as_str() {
273	                    Some("text") => {
274	                        if let Some(t) = b["text"].as_str() {
275	                            if !t.trim().is_empty() { out.push(UserPart::Text(t.to_string())); }
276	                        }
277	                    }
278	                    Some("tool_result") => {
279	                        let id = b["tool_use_id"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
280	                        let name = tool_names.get(&id).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| "tool".to_string());
281	                        let is_error = b["is_error"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false);
282	                        let content = match &b["content"] {
283	                            Value::String(s) => s.clone(),
284	                            Value::Array(arr) => arr.iter()
285	                                .filter(|x| x["type"].as_str() == Some("text"))
286	                                .filter_map(|x| x["text"].as_str())
287	                                .collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n"),
288	                            _ => String::new(),
289	                        };
290	                        out.push(UserPart::ToolResult { id, name, content, is_error });
291	                    }
292	                    _ => {}
293	                }
294	            }
295	        }
296	        _ => {}
297	    }
298	    out
299	}
300	
301	fn parse_asst(content: &Value) -> Vec<AsstPart> {
302	    let mut out = Vec::new();
303	    match content {
304	        Value::String(s) if !s.trim().is_empty() => out.push(AsstPart::Text(s.clone())),
305	        Value::Array(blocks) => {
306	            for b in blocks {
307	                match b["type"].as_str() {
308	                    Some("text") => {
309	                        if let Some(t) = b["text"].as_str() {
310	                            if !t.trim().is_empty() { out.push(AsstPart::Text(t.to_string())); }
311	                        }
312	                    }
313	                    Some("tool_use") => {
314	                        out.push(AsstPart::ToolCall {
315	                            id:     b["id"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
316	                            name:   b["name"].as_str().unwrap_or("unknown").to_string(),
317	                            input:  b["input"].clone(),
318	                            result: None,
319	                        });
320	                    }
321	                    _ => {}
322	                }
323	            }
324	        }
325	        _ => {}
326	    }
327	    out
328	}
329	
330	// ── Text extraction (for clipboard) ──────────────────────────────────────────
331	
332	fn card_text(card: &Card) -> String {
333	    let mut out = String::new();
334	    match card {
335	        Card::RepoLink { name, dir, branch } => {
336	            out.push_str(name); out.push('\n');
337	            out.push_str(dir);  out.push('\n');
338	            if !branch.is_empty() { out.push_str(branch); out.push('\n'); }
339	        }
340	        Card::Header { title, cwd, branch, agent, .. } => {
341	            if let Some(t) = title { out.push_str(t); out.push('\n'); }
342	            if !cwd.is_empty()    { out.push_str(cwd);               out.push('\n'); }
343	            if !branch.is_empty() { out.push_str(&format!("[{branch}]")); out.push('\n'); }
344	            if !agent.is_empty()  { out.push_str(agent); out.push('\n'); }
345	        }
346	        Card::System { content, .. } => { if !content.is_empty() { out.push_str(content); out.push('\n'); } }
347	        Card::UserMsg { parts, .. } => {
348	            for part in parts {
349	                match part {
350	                    UserPart::Text(t) => { out.push_str(t); out.push('\n'); }
351	                    UserPart::ToolResult { name, content, .. } => {
352	                        out.push_str(&format!("[Result: {name}]\n{content}\n"));
353	                    }
354	                }
355	            }
356	        }
357	        Card::AsstMsg { parts, .. } => {
358	            for part in parts {
359	                if let AsstPart::Text(t) = part { out.push_str(t); out.push('\n'); }
360	            }
361	        }
362	        Card::ToolRound { parts } => {
363	            for part in parts {
364	                if let AsstPart::ToolCall { name, input, result, .. } = part {
365	                    out.push_str(&format!("[Tool: {name}]\n"));
366	                    if let Some(obj) = input.as_object() {
367	                        for (k, v) in obj {
368	                            let val = if let Value::String(s) = v { s.clone() } else { v.to_string() };
369	                            out.push_str(&format!("{k}: {val}\n"));
370	                        }
371	                    }
372	                    if let Some((content, _)) = result {
373	                        out.push_str(&format!("[Result]\n{content}\n"));
374	                    }
375	                }
376	            }
377	        }
378	    }
379	    out
380	}
381	
382	fn tool_call_text(part: &AsstPart) -> String {
383	    let mut out = String::new();
384	    if let AsstPart::ToolCall { name, input, result, .. } = part {
385	        out.push_str(&format!("[Tool: {name}]\n"));
386	        if let Some(obj) = input.as_object() {
387	            for (k, v) in obj {
388	                let val = if let Value::String(s) = v { s.clone() } else { v.to_string() };
389	                out.push_str(&format!("{k}: {val}\n"));
390	            }
391	        }
392	        if let Some((content, _)) = result { out.push_str(&format!("[Result]\n{content}\n")); }
393	    }
394	    out
395	}
396	
397	fn copy_to_clipboard(text: &str) {
398	    use std::io::Write as _;
399	    use std::process::{Command, Stdio};
400	    if let Ok(mut child) = Command::new("pbcopy").stdin(Stdio::piped()).spawn() {
401	        if let Some(stdin) = child.stdin.take() {
402	            let mut stdin = stdin;
403	            let _ = stdin.write_all(text.as_bytes());
404	        }
405	        let _ = child.wait();
406	    }
407	}
408	
409	// ── Markdown rendering ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
410	
411	static MD_SKIN: OnceLock<termimad::MadSkin> = OnceLock::new();
412	
413	fn md_skin() -> &'static termimad::MadSkin {
414	    MD_SKIN.get_or_init(|| {
415	        use crossterm::style::{Attribute, Color};
416	        let mut skin = termimad::MadSkin::default();
417	        // Bright white bold
418	        skin.bold.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(255));
419	        skin.bold.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);
420	        // Italic — subtle light color
421	        skin.italic.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(252));
422	        skin.italic.add_attr(Attribute::Italic);
423	        // Inline code — cyan, no background
424	        skin.inline_code.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(116));
425	        skin.inline_code.object_style.background_color = None;
426	        // Code blocks — similar cyan, no background
427	        skin.code_block.compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(116));
428	        skin.code_block.compound_style.object_style.background_color = None;
429	        // Normal paragraph text — light grey
430	        skin.paragraph.compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(252));
431	        // Headers — bright
432	        skin.headers[0].compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(229));
433	        skin.headers[0].compound_style.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);
434	        skin.headers[1].compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(222));
435	        skin.headers[1].compound_style.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);
436	        skin.headers[2].compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(216));
437	        skin.headers[2].compound_style.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);
438	        // Bullet list markers — same as body
439	        skin.bullet.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(252));
440	        skin
441	    })
442	}
443	
444	fn render_md(text: &str, width: usize) -> Vec<String> {
445	    let rendered = format!("{}", md_skin().text(text, Some(width)));
446	    // termimad appends a trailing newline; drop it to avoid spurious blank lines
447	    let trimmed = rendered.trim_end_matches('\n');
448	    trimmed.lines().map(str::to_string).collect()
449	}
```

```
571	                let col = if is_first { "38;5;255" } else { "38;5;245" };

582	            let visible: Vec<&str> = content.lines()
583	                .filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty()).take(8).collect();
584	            for l in wrap(&visible.join("\n"), w.saturating_sub(4)) {
585	                lines.push(format!("\x1b[{col}m    {l}\x1b[0m"));
586	            }
587	            let total = content.lines().filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty()).count();

590	            }
591	        }
592	        lines.push(String::new());
593	    }
594	    lines
595	}

597	// Visible character width, skipping ANSI escape sequences.
598	fn visible_width(s: &str) -> usize {
599	    let mut w = 0usize;
600	    let mut chars = s.chars().peekable();
601	    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
602	        if c == '\x1b' {
603	            // consume up to and including the final byte of the CSI sequence
604	            for nc in chars.by_ref() {
605	                if nc.is_ascii_alphabetic() { break; }
606	            }
607	        } else {
608	            w += 1;
609	        }
610	    }
611	    w
612	}
613	
614	// Apply a selection background to a pre-colored ANSI string.
615	// Parses SGR sequences properly so that any explicit background color emitted
616	// by termimad (e.g. `\x1b[48;5;238m` for inline code) is replaced with the
617	// selection background rather than overriding it.
618	fn with_bg(s: &str, bg: &str) -> String {
619	    let bg_seq  = format!("\x1b[{bg}m");
620	    let mut out = bg_seq.clone();
621	    let bytes   = s.as_bytes();
622	    let mut i   = 0;
623	
624	    while i < bytes.len() {
625	        if bytes[i] == b'\x1b' && bytes.get(i + 1) == Some(&b'[') {
626	            // Parse CSI sequence: ESC [ <params> <cmd>
627	            let esc_start   = i;
628	            i += 2;
629	            let params_start = i;
630	            while i < bytes.len() && !bytes[i].is_ascii_alphabetic() { i += 1; }
631	            let cmd    = *bytes.get(i).unwrap_or(&b'm');
632	            let params = &s[params_start..i];
633	            i += 1;
634	
635	            if cmd == b'm' {
636	                out.push_str(&rewrite_sgr(params, &bg_seq));
637	            } else {
638	                out.push_str(&s[esc_start..i]);
639	            }
640	        } else {
641	            let ch_len = s[i..].chars().next().map_or(1, |c| c.len_utf8());
642	            out.push_str(&s[i..i + ch_len]);
643	            i += ch_len;
644	        }
645	    }
646	
647	    out
648	}
649	
650	// Rewrite a single SGR parameter string so that any background colour is
651	// replaced with the selection background. Foreground / attribute codes are
652	// kept; a pure reset gets the background re-appended.
653	fn rewrite_sgr(params: &str, bg_seq: &str) -> String {
654	    if params.is_empty() || params == "0" {
655	        return format!("\x1b[0m{bg_seq}");
656	    }
657	
658	    let mut non_bg: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();
659	    let mut found_bg = false;
660	    let mut segs = params.split(';').peekable();
661	
662	    while let Some(seg) = segs.next() {
663	        match seg {
664	            // Standard background colours 40-47 and bright 100-107
665	            "40"|"41"|"42"|"43"|"44"|"45"|"46"|"47"
666	            |"100"|"101"|"102"|"103"|"104"|"105"|"106"|"107" => {
667	                found_bg = true;
668	            }
669	            // 256-colour or true-colour background: 48;5;N or 48;2;R;G;B
670	            "48" => {
671	                found_bg = true;
672	                match segs.next().as_deref() {
673	                    Some("5") => { segs.next(); }          // skip N
674	                    Some("2") => { segs.next(); segs.next(); segs.next(); } // skip R;G;B
675	                    _ => {}
676	                }
677	            }
678	            // \x1b[49m — reset background to default (termimad uses this, not \x1b[0m)
679	            "49" => { found_bg = true; }
680	            // Reset within a combined sequence — keep it
681	            "0" => non_bg.push("0"),
682	            other => non_bg.push(other),
683	        }
684	    }
685	
686	    if !found_bg {
687	        return format!("\x1b[{params}m");
688	    }
689	
690	    let mut result = String::new();
691	    if !non_bg.is_empty() {
692	        result.push_str(&format!("\x1b[{}m", non_bg.join(";")));
693	    }
694	    result.push_str(bg_seq);
695	    result
696	}
697	
698	fn wrap(text: &str, width: usize) -> Vec<String> {
699	    if width < 4 { return text.lines().map(str::to_string).collect(); }
700	    let mut out = Vec::new();
701	    for raw_line in text.lines() {
702	        if raw_line.is_empty() { out.push(String::new()); continue; }
703	        let mut remaining = raw_line;
704	        loop {
705	            if remaining.chars().count() <= width {
706	                out.push(remaining.to_string());
707	                break;
708	            }
709	            let break_pos = remaining.chars().take(width)
710	                .collect::<String>()
711	                .rfind(' ')
712	                .unwrap_or(width);
713	            let byte_pos = remaining.char_indices().nth(break_pos)
714	                .map(|(i, _)| i)
715	                .unwrap_or(remaining.len());
716	            out.push(remaining[..byte_pos].to_string());
717	            remaining = remaining[byte_pos..].trim_start_matches(' ');
718	        }
719	    }
720	    out
721	}
722	
723	fn rel_time(iso: &str) -> String {
724	    let Ok(dt) = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(iso) else { return iso.to_string(); };
725	    let secs = (Utc::now() - dt.with_timezone(&Utc)).num_seconds().max(0);
726	    if secs < 604_800 {
727	        match secs {
728	            s if s < 60     => "just now".into(),
729	            s if s < 3_600  => format!("{} min{} ago", s/60,     if s/60==1     {""} else {"s"}),
730	            s if s < 86_400 => format!("{} hr{} ago",  s/3_600,  if s/3_600==1  {""} else {"s"}),
731	            s               => format!("{} day{} ago", s/86_400, if s/86_400==1 {""} else {"s"}),
732	        }
733	    } else {
734	        dt.with_timezone(&Local).format("%m/%d/%y").to_string()
735	    }
736	}
737	
738	// ── Resume logic ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
739	
740	fn git_current_branch(cwd: &str) -> Option<String> {
741	    let out = std::process::Command::new("git")
742	        .args(["rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"])
743	        .current_dir(cwd)
744	        .output()
745	        .ok()?;
746	    if !out.status.success() { return None; }
747	    let s = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).trim().to_string();
748	    if s.is_empty() || s == "HEAD" { None } else { Some(s) }
749	}
750	
751	fn create_resume_worktree(cwd: &str, branch: &str) -> Option<String> {
752	    let root_out = std::process::Command::new("git")
753	        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
754	        .current_dir(cwd)
755	        .output()
756	        .ok()?;
757	    if !root_out.status.success() { return None; }
758	    let repo_root = String::from_utf8_lossy(&root_out.stdout).trim().to_string();
759	
760	    let repo_path = std::path::Path::new(&repo_root);
761	    let parent = repo_path.parent()?;
762	    let repo_name = repo_path.file_name()?.to_str()?;
763	    let wt_path = parent.join(format!("{repo_name}-{branch}"));
764	
765	    // Branch already exists; add a worktree pointing at it.
766	    let _ = std::process::Command::new("git")
767	        .args(["worktree", "add", wt_path.to_str()?, branch])
768	        .current_dir(&repo_root)
769	        .output();
770	
771	    if wt_path.exists() { Some(wt_path.to_string_lossy().to_string()) } else { None }
772	}
773	
774	fn resume_via_entire(dir: &str, branch: &str) {
775	    use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
776	
777	    let launch_dir = if !branch.is_empty() {
778	        let current = git_current_branch(dir);
779	        if current.as_deref() != Some(branch) {
780	            create_resume_worktree(dir, branch).unwrap_or_else(|| dir.to_string())
781	        } else {
782	            dir.to_string()
783	        }
784	    } else {
785	        dir.to_string()
786	    };
787	
788	    let mut entire = std::process::Command::new("entire");
789	    entire.arg("resume").current_dir(&launch_dir);
790	    if !branch.is_empty() { entire.arg(branch); }
791	
792	    let Ok(out) = entire.output() else { eprintln!("entire resume failed"); return; };
793	
794	    let cmd_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).trim().to_string();
795	    if cmd_str.is_empty() { eprintln!("entire resume returned no command"); return; }
796	
797	    let mut parts = cmd_str.split_whitespace();
798	    let Some(prog) = parts.next() else { return; };
799	    let args: Vec<&str> = parts.collect();
800	    let err = std::process::Command::new(prog).args(&args).current_dir(&launch_dir).exec();
801	    eprintln!("exec failed: {err}");
802	}
803	
804	/// Find a Claude session by ID. Returns `(cwd, git_branch)` from the JSONL if found,
805	/// so `claude --resume` can be run from the correct directory and branch is known.
806	fn find_claude_session_cwd(session_id: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
807	    use std::io::BufRead;
808	    let home = std::env::var("HOME").ok()?;
809	    let projects = std::path::PathBuf::from(&home).join(".claude/projects");
810	    for dir in std::fs::read_dir(&projects).ok()?.flatten() {
811	        let jsonl = dir.path().join(format!("{session_id}.jsonl"));
812	        if !jsonl.exists() { continue; }
813	        let Ok(file) = std::fs::File::open(&jsonl) else { return Some((String::new(), String::new())); };
814	        let mut cwd = String::new();
815	        let mut branch = String::new();
816	        for line in std::io::BufReader::new(file).lines().flatten() {
817	            let Ok(v) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&line) else { continue };
818	            if matches!(v["type"].as_str(), Some("user") | Some("system")) {
819	                if cwd.is_empty() {
820	                    if let Some(c) = v["cwd"].as_str() { cwd = c.to_string(); }
821	                }
822	                if branch.is_empty() {
823	                    if let Some(b) = v["gitBranch"].as_str() { branch = b.to_string(); }
824	                }
825	                if !cwd.is_empty() && !branch.is_empty() { break; }
826	            }
827	        }
828	        return Some((cwd, branch));
829	    }
830	    None
831	}
832	
833	fn do_resume(_agent: &str, session_id: &str, session_branch: &str, session_cwd: &str) {
834	    let current = git_current_branch(session_cwd);
835	    let same_branch = !session_branch.is_empty()
836	        && current.as_deref() == Some(session_branch);
837	
838	    let dir = if same_branch || session_branch.is_empty() {
839	        session_cwd.to_string()
840	    } else {
841	        create_resume_worktree(session_cwd, session_branch)
842	            .unwrap_or_else(|| session_cwd.to_string())
843	    };
844	
845	    resume_session(session_id, &dir);
846	}
847	
848	/// Resume a session by ID, falling back to `entire resume` if the Claude JSONL is absent.
849	/// `fallback_cwd` is used for `entire resume` if the Claude session isn't found.
850	pub fn resume_session(session_id: &str, fallback_cwd: &str) {
851	    use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
852	    if let Some((session_cwd, session_branch)) = find_claude_session_cwd(session_id) {
853	        let launch_dir = if session_cwd.is_empty() || !std::path::Path::new(&session_cwd).exists() {
854	            fallback_cwd.to_string()
855	        } else {
856	            session_cwd
857	        };
858	        let err = std::process::Command::new("claude")
859	            .arg("--resume")
860	            .arg(session_id)
861	            .current_dir(&launch_dir)
862	            .exec();
863	        eprintln!("exec failed: {err}");
864	        // exec failed — fall back to entire resume, checking out the session branch first
865	        resume_via_entire(&launch_dir, &session_branch);
866	    } else {
867	        resume_via_entire(fallback_cwd, "");
868	    }
869	}
870	
871	// ── Pager ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
872	
873	fn build_flat(
874	    cards: &[Card],
875	    term_w: usize,
876	    collapsed: &std::collections::HashSet<usize>,
877	) -> (Vec<(usize, String)>, Vec<Selectable>, Vec<usize>) {
878	    let w = term_w.saturating_sub(2);
879	    let mut flat: Vec<(usize, String)> = Vec::new();
880	    let mut selectables: Vec<Selectable> = Vec::new();
881	    let mut starts: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
882	
883	    // Pull the agent name from the Header card so render_card can use it.
884	    let agent: &str = cards.iter().find_map(|c| {
885	        if let Card::Header { agent, .. } = c { Some(agent.as_str()) } else { None }
886	    }).unwrap_or("");
887	
888	    for (card_idx, card) in cards.iter().enumerate() {
889	        if let Card::ToolRound { parts } = card {
890	            if collapsed.contains(&card_idx) {
891	                let si = selectables.len();
892	                starts.push(flat.len());
893	                selectables.push(Selectable::Card(card_idx));
894	                for l in render_tool_summary(parts) { flat.push((si, l)); }
895	            } else {
896	                // Header row (collapses the round when space is pressed)
897	                let si = selectables.len();
898	                starts.push(flat.len());
899	                selectables.push(Selectable::ToolHeader(card_idx));
900	                for l in render_tool_header(parts) { flat.push((si, l)); }
901	                // One selectable per individual tool call
902	                for (tool_idx, part) in parts.iter().enumerate() {
903	                    let si = selectables.len();
904	                    starts.push(flat.len());
905	                    selectables.push(Selectable::ToolCall(card_idx, tool_idx));
906	                    for l in render_one_tool_call(part, w) { flat.push((si, l)); }
907	                }
908	            }
909	        } else {
910	            let si = selectables.len();
911	            starts.push(flat.len());
912	            selectables.push(Selectable::Card(card_idx));
913	            for l in render_card(card, w, agent) { flat.push((si, l)); }
914	        }
915	    }
916	
917	    (flat, selectables, starts)
918	}
919	
920	enum PagerOutcome { Quit, Resume, Delete, GoToSessions, GoToRepo(String) }
921	
922	fn pager(cards: &[Card], start_ts: Option<&str>) -> Result<PagerOutcome> {
923	    let (term_w, term_h) = terminal::size().unwrap_or((120, 40));
924	    let mut w = term_w as usize;
925	    let mut h = (term_h as usize).saturating_sub(1);
926	
927	    let mut collapsed: std::collections::HashSet<usize> = cards.iter().enumerate()
928	        .filter_map(|(i, c)| if matches!(c, Card::ToolRound { .. }) { Some(i) } else { None })
929	        .collect();
930	
931	    let (mut flat, mut selectables, mut starts) = build_flat(&cards, w, &collapsed);
932	
933	    let mut stdout = io::stdout();
934	
935	    let orig_hook = std::panic::take_hook();
936	    std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |info| {
937	        let mut out = io::stdout();
938	        let _ = execute!(out, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show);
939	        let _ = terminal::disable_raw_mode();
940	        orig_hook(info);
941	    }));
942	
943	    terminal::enable_raw_mode()?;
944	    execute!(stdout, EnterAlternateScreen, cursor::Hide)?;
945	
946	    // If a target timestamp was given, jump to the first card whose ts matches.
947	    // Otherwise land on the first non-navigation card (skip RepoLink and Header).
948	    let initial_sel = start_ts.and_then(|ts| {
949	        cards.iter().enumerate().find_map(|(ci, card)| {
950	            let card_ts = match card {
951	                Card::UserMsg { ts, .. } | Card::AsstMsg { ts, .. } | Card::System { ts, .. } => ts.as_str(),
952	                _ => return None,
953	            };
954	            if card_ts == ts {
955	                selectables.iter().position(|s| *s == Selectable::Card(ci))
956	            } else {
957	                None
958	            }
959	        })
960	    }).unwrap_or_else(|| selectables.len().saturating_sub(1));
961	
962	    let mut sel: usize = initial_sel;
963	    let mut scroll: usize = 0;
964	    let mut flash:  Option<&str> = None;
965	    let mut awaiting_delete = false;
966	
967	    let result: Result<PagerOutcome> = loop {
968	        let s = starts[sel];
969	        let e = starts.get(sel + 1).copied().unwrap_or(flat.len());
970	        if s < scroll          { scroll = s; }
971	        else if e > scroll + h { scroll = e.saturating_sub(h); }
972	
973	        if let Err(err) = draw(&mut stdout, &flat, &starts, sel, scroll, h, w, selectables.len(), flash) {
974	            break Err(anyhow::anyhow!(err));
975	        }
976	
977	        match event::read() {
978	            Err(e) => break Err(e.into()),
979	            Ok(Event::Key(k)) => {
980	                let prev_flash = flash.take();
981	                match (k.code, k.modifiers) {
982	                    (KeyCode::Char('q') | KeyCode::Esc | KeyCode::Left, _) => break Ok(PagerOutcome::Quit),
983	                    (KeyCode::Char('c'), KeyModifiers::CONTROL) => break Ok(PagerOutcome::Quit),
984	                    (KeyCode::Char('r'), _) => break Ok(PagerOutcome::Resume),
985	                    (KeyCode::Char('d'), _) => {
986	                        awaiting_delete = true;
987	                        flash = Some("  Delete session? Press y to confirm, any other key to cancel  ");
988	                        continue;
989	                    }
990	                    (KeyCode::Char('y'), _) if awaiting_delete => {
991	                        break Ok(PagerOutcome::Delete);
992	                    }
993	
994	                    (KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j'), _) => {
995	                        if sel + 1 < selectables.len() { sel += 1; }
996	                    }
997	                    (KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k'), _) => {
998	                        if sel > 0 { sel -= 1; }
999	                    }
1000	                    (KeyCode::Char('g'), _) => { sel = 0; }
1001	                    (KeyCode::Char('G'), _) => { sel = selectables.len().saturating_sub(1); }
1002	                    (KeyCode::Char('u'), _) | (KeyCode::PageUp, _) => {
1003	                        scroll = scroll.saturating_sub(h / 2);
1004	                        sel = flat[scroll].0;
1005	                    }
1006	                    (KeyCode::PageDown, _) => {
1007	                        scroll = (scroll + h / 2).min(flat.len().saturating_sub(h));
1008	                        sel = flat[scroll].0;
1009	                    }
1010	
1011	                    (KeyCode::Char('y'), _) | (KeyCode::Char('c'), _) => {
1012	                        let text = match &selectables[sel] {
1013	                            Selectable::Card(ci)         => card_text(&cards[*ci]),
1014	                            Selectable::ToolHeader(ci)   => card_text(&cards[*ci]),
1015	                            Selectable::ToolCall(ci, ti) => {
1016	                                if let Card::ToolRound { parts } = &cards[*ci] {
1017	                                    parts.get(*ti).map_or(String::new(), tool_call_text)
1018	                                } else { String::new() }
1019	                            }
1020	                        };
1021	                        copy_to_clipboard(&text);
1022	                        flash = Some("  ✓ copied to clipboard  ");
1023	                    }
1024	
1025	                    // Space/Right/Enter: expand ToolRound, collapse via header, or navigate
1026	                    (KeyCode::Char(' ') | KeyCode::Right | KeyCode::Enter, _) => {
1027	                        let expand_action = match &selectables[sel] {
1028	                            Selectable::Card(ci) if matches!(&cards[*ci], Card::ToolRound { .. }) => {
1029	                                Some((true, *ci))
1030	                            }
1031	                            Selectable::ToolHeader(ci) => Some((false, *ci)),
1032	                            _ => None,
1033	                        };
1034	                        if let Some((expand, card_idx)) = expand_action {
1035	                            if expand { collapsed.remove(&card_idx); } else { collapsed.insert(card_idx); }
1036	                            let (nf, ns, nst) = build_flat(cards, w, &collapsed);
1037	                            flat   = nf;
1038	                            starts = nst;
1039	                            sel = if expand {
1040	                                ns.iter().position(|s| *s == Selectable::ToolHeader(card_idx))
1041	                            } else {
1042	                                ns.iter().position(|s| *s == Selectable::Card(card_idx))
1043	                            }.unwrap_or_else(|| sel.min(ns.len().saturating_sub(1)));
1044	                            selectables = ns;
1045	                        } else if let Selectable::Card(ci) = &selectables[sel] {
1046	                            match &cards[*ci] {
1047	                                Card::RepoLink { dir, .. } => {
1048	                                    break Ok(PagerOutcome::GoToRepo(dir.clone()));
1049	                                }
1050	                                Card::Header { .. } => {
1051	                                    break Ok(PagerOutcome::GoToSessions);
1052	                                }
1053	                                _ => { flash = prev_flash; }
1054	                            }
1055	                        } else {
1056	                            flash = prev_flash;
1057	                        }
1058	                    }
1059	
1060	                    _ => { awaiting_delete = false; flash = prev_flash; }
1061	                }
1062	            }
1063	            Ok(Event::Resize(new_w, new_h)) => {
1064	                w = new_w as usize;
1065	                h = (new_h as usize).saturating_sub(1);
1066	                let (nf, ns, nst) = build_flat(&cards, w, &collapsed);
1067	                flat = nf; selectables = ns; starts = nst;
1068	                execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::All)).ok();
1069	            }
1070	            Ok(_) => {}
1071	        }
1072	    };
1073	
1074	    execute!(stdout, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show)?;
1075	    terminal::disable_raw_mode()?;
1076	    result
1077	}
1078	
1079	fn draw(
1080	    stdout: &mut impl Write,
1081	    flat:   &[(usize, String)],
1082	    starts: &[usize],
1083	    sel:    usize,
1084	    scroll: usize,
1085	    h:      usize,
1086	    w:      usize,
1087	    total:  usize,
1088	    flash:  Option<&str>,
1089	) -> io::Result<()> {
1090	    use crossterm::queue;
1091	    const SEL_BG: &str = "48;5;236";
1092	
1093	    let end = (scroll + h).min(flat.len());
1094	
1095	    // Build the entire frame into one buffer; flush once to eliminate flicker.
1096	    // Use explicit cursor::MoveTo per row so embedded newlines in content can't
1097	    // shift subsequent rows into the wrong position.
1098	    let mut buf: Vec<u8> = Vec::with_capacity((w + 40) * (h + 2));
1099	
1100	    for row in 0..h {
1101	        queue!(buf, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16), terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
1102	
1103	        let flat_idx = scroll + row;
1104	        if flat_idx < end {
1105	            let (card_idx, line) = &flat[flat_idx];
1106	            if *card_idx == sel {
1107	                let line_bg = with_bg(line, SEL_BG);
1108	                let vis = visible_width(line);
1109	                let pad = w.saturating_sub(vis);
1110	                write!(buf, "\x1b[{SEL_BG}m{line_bg}{}\x1b[0m", " ".repeat(pad))?;
1111	            } else {
1112	                buf.extend_from_slice(line.as_bytes());
1113	            }
1114	        }
1115	    }
1116	
1117	    // Status bar
1118	    let sel_end = starts.get(sel + 1).copied().unwrap_or(flat.len());
1119	    let base = format!(
1120	        "  {}/{} msgs  lines {}-{}  j/k ↑↓ navigate  u/PgDn page  g/G ends  y/c copy  r resume  d delete  q quit  ",
1121	        sel + 1, total, starts[sel] + 1, sel_end,
1122	    );
1123	    let bar = if let Some(msg) = flash {
1124	        let skip = msg.chars().count();
1125	        let rest: String = base.chars().skip(skip).collect();
1126	        format!("{msg}{rest}")
1127	    } else {
1128	        base
1129	    };
1130	    let bar_display: String = bar.chars().take(w).collect();
1131	    let padded = format!("{bar_display:width$}", width = w);
1132	    queue!(buf, cursor::MoveTo(0, h as u16))?;
1133	    write!(buf, "\x1b[7m{padded}\x1b[0m")?;
1134	
1135	    stdout.write_all(&buf)?;
1136	    stdout.flush()
1137	}
1138	
```

```
173	// ── Hit parsing ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
174	
175	fn parse_hit(metadata_json: &str, bodies: &[String], sub_idx: u32) -> SearchHit {
176	    let sub_idx = sub_idx as usize;
177	    let meta: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(metadata_json).unwrap_or_default();
178	    let source = meta["source"].as_str().unwrap_or("claude");
179	
180	    match source {
181	        "session" => {
182	            let name = meta["session_name"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
183	            let cwd  = meta["cwd"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
184	            SearchHit {
185	                kind: HitKind::Session,
186	                title: name,
187	                dir: short_path(&cwd),
188	                excerpt_lines: vec![],
189	                session_id: meta["session_id"].as_str().map(str::to_string),
190	                repo_dir: None,
191	                start_ts: None, hit_ts: None, branch: String::new(),
192	                agent: String::new(), backed_up: false, updated_at: String::new(),
193	                remote: String::new(),
194	            }
195	        }
196	        "repo" => {
197	            let name = meta["repo_name"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
198	            let dir  = meta["repo_dir"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
199	            SearchHit {
200	                kind: HitKind::Repo,
201	                title: name,
202	                dir: short_path(&dir),
203	                excerpt_lines: vec![],
204	                session_id: None,
205	                repo_dir: Some(dir),
206	                start_ts: None, hit_ts: None, branch: String::new(),
207	                agent: String::new(), backed_up: false, updated_at: String::new(),
208	                remote: String::new(),
209	            }
210	        }
211	        _ => {
212	            // "claude" — session log turn
213	            let name   = meta["session_name"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
214	            let project = meta["project"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
215	            let branch  = meta["branch"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
216	
217	            // Collect context: previous turn, matched turn, next turn.
218	            // bodies[0] is the header; bodies[N] for N>0 is a conversation turn.
219	            let mut excerpt_lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
220	            if sub_idx > 1 {
221	                if let Some(prev) = bodies.get(sub_idx - 1) {
222	                    let t: String = prev.trim().chars().take(220).collect();
223	                    if !t.is_empty() { excerpt_lines.push(t); }
224	                }
225	            }
226	            if let Some(matched) = bodies.get(sub_idx) {
227	                let t: String = matched.trim().chars().take(400).collect();
228	                if !t.is_empty() { excerpt_lines.push(t); }
229	            }
230	            if sub_idx + 1 < bodies.len() {
231	                if let Some(next) = bodies.get(sub_idx + 1) {
232	                    let t: String = next.trim().chars().take(220).collect();
233	                    if !t.is_empty() { excerpt_lines.push(t); }
234	                }
235	            }
236	
237	            let hit_ts = if sub_idx > 0 {
238	                meta["turns"][sub_idx - 1]["timestamp"].as_str()
239	                    .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
240	                    .map(str::to_string)
241	            } else {
242	                None
243	            };
244	
245	            SearchHit {
246	                kind: HitKind::Log,
247	                title: name,
248	                dir: short_path(&project),
249	                excerpt_lines,
250	                session_id: meta["session_id"].as_str().map(str::to_string),
251	                repo_dir: None,
252	                start_ts: hit_ts.clone(),
253	                hit_ts,
254	                branch,
255	                agent: String::new(), backed_up: false, updated_at: String::new(),
256	                remote: String::new(),
257	            }
258	        }
259	    }
260	}
261	
262	// ── Group building ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
263	
264	fn build_groups(hits: Vec<SearchHit>) -> Vec<Group> {
265	    let mut groups: Vec<Group> = Vec::new();
266	    let mut log_group_idx: std::collections::HashMap<String, usize> = std::collections::HashMap::new();
267	
268	    for hit in hits {
269	        match hit.kind {
270	            HitKind::Log => {
271	                let sid = hit.session_id.clone().unwrap_or_default();
272	                if let Some(&gi) = log_group_idx.get(&sid) {
273	                    groups[gi].rows.push(GroupRow {
274	                        hit_ts: hit.hit_ts,
275	                        start_ts: hit.start_ts,
276	                        lines: hit.excerpt_lines,
277	                    });
278	                } else {
279	                    let gi = groups.len();
280	                    if !sid.is_empty() { log_group_idx.insert(sid, gi); }
281	                    groups.push(Group {
282	                        title: hit.title,
283	                        dir: hit.dir,
284	                        branch: hit.branch,
285	                        agent: hit.agent,
286	                        backed_up: hit.backed_up,
287	                        updated_at: hit.updated_at,
288	                        remote: hit.remote,
289	                        session_id: hit.session_id,
290	                        repo_dir: None,
291	                        kind: HitKind::Log,
292	                        rows: vec![GroupRow {
293	                            hit_ts: hit.hit_ts,
294	                            start_ts: hit.start_ts,
295	                            lines: hit.excerpt_lines,
296	                        }],
297	                    });
298	                }
299	            }
300	            HitKind::Session => {
301	                groups.push(Group {
302	                    title: hit.title,
303	                    dir: hit.dir,
304	                    branch: String::new(),
305	                    agent: hit.agent,
306	                    backed_up: hit.backed_up,
307	                    updated_at: hit.updated_at,
308	                    remote: hit.remote,
309	                    session_id: hit.session_id,
310	                    repo_dir: None,
311	                    kind: HitKind::Session,
312	                    rows: vec![],
313	                });
314	            }
315	            HitKind::Repo => {
316	                groups.push(Group {
317	                    title: hit.title,
318	                    dir: hit.dir,
319	                    branch: String::new(),
320	                    agent: String::new(),
321	                    backed_up: false,
322	                    updated_at: String::new(),
323	                    remote: hit.remote,
324	                    session_id: None,
325	                    repo_dir: hit.repo_dir,
326	                    kind: HitKind::Repo,
327	                    rows: vec![],
328	                });
329	            }
330	        }
331	    }
332	
333	    groups
334	}
335	
336	// ── TUI ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
337	
338	fn tui_loop(stdout: &mut impl Write, groups: &[Group], query: &str, ms: u128) -> Result<()> {
339	    let mut sel    = 0usize;
340	    let mut scroll = 0usize;
341	
342	    loop {
343	        let (w, h) = terminal::size().unwrap_or((120, 40));
344	        let w = w as usize;
345	        let h = h as usize;
346	        let content_h = h.saturating_sub(2);
347	
348	        let sel_start = group_start_of(groups, sel);
349	        let sel_end   = sel_start + groups.get(sel).map_or(1, rows_for_group);
350	        if sel_start < scroll                { scroll = sel_start; }
351	        else if sel_end > scroll + content_h { scroll = sel_end.saturating_sub(content_h); }
352	
353	        draw(stdout, groups, query, ms, sel, scroll, w, h)?;
354	
355	        match event::read()? {
356	            Event::Key(k) => match k.code {
357	                KeyCode::Char('q') | KeyCode::Esc => break,
358	                KeyCode::Char('c') if k.modifiers.contains(crossterm::event::KeyModifiers::CONTROL) => {
359	                    execute!(stdout, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show).ok();
360	                    terminal::disable_raw_mode().ok();
361	                    std::process::exit(0);
362	                }
363	                KeyCode::Up   | KeyCode::Char('k') => { if sel > 0 { sel -= 1; } }
364	                KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => { if sel + 1 < groups.len() { sel += 1; } }
365	                KeyCode::Char('g') => { sel = 0; scroll = 0; }
366	                KeyCode::Char('G') => { sel = groups.len().saturating_sub(1); }
367	                KeyCode::Char(' ') | KeyCode::Right | KeyCode::Enter => {
368	                    if let Some(group) = groups.get(sel) {
369	                        execute!(stdout, LeaveAlternateScreen, cursor::Show).ok();
370	                        terminal::disable_raw_mode().ok();
371	
372	                        match group.kind {
373	                            HitKind::Log | HitKind::Session => {
374	                                if let Some(id) = &group.session_id {
375	                                    let ts = group.rows.first().and_then(|r| r.start_ts.as_deref());
376	                                    let _ = super::show::run_at(id, ts);
377	                                }
378	                            }
379	                            HitKind::Repo => {
380	                                if let Some(dir) = &group.repo_dir {
381	                                    let _ = super::status::run_for_dir(dir);
382	                                }
383	                            }
384	                        }
385	
386	                        terminal::enable_raw_mode().ok();
387	                        execute!(stdout, EnterAlternateScreen, cursor::Hide).ok();
388	                        execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::All)).ok();
389	                    }
390	                }
391	                _ => {}
392	            },
393	            Event::Resize(_, _) => {
394	                execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::All)).ok();
395	            }
396	            _ => {}
397	        }
398	    }
399	
400	    Ok(())
401	}
402	
403	fn rows_for_group(g: &Group) -> usize {
404	    1 + g.rows.iter().map(|r| r.lines.len().max(1)).sum::<usize>() + 1 // +1 for blank separator
405	}
406	
407	fn group_start_of(groups: &[Group], idx: usize) -> usize {
408	    groups[..idx.min(groups.len())].iter().map(rows_for_group).sum()
409	}
410	
411	// ── Rendering ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
412	
413	fn draw(
414	    stdout: &mut impl Write,
415	    groups: &[Group],
416	    query:  &str,
417	    ms:     u128,
418	    sel:    usize,
419	    scroll: usize,
420	    w:      usize,
421	    h:      usize,
422	) -> io::Result<()> {
423	    use crossterm::queue;
424	    const SEL_BG: &str  = "48;5;236";
425	    const TS_W:   usize = 12; // fixed width of the timestamp column in excerpt rows
426	
427	    let content_h = h.saturating_sub(2);
428	    let mut buf: Vec<u8> = Vec::with_capacity((w + 60) * (h + 2));
429	
430	    // Title bar
431	    queue!(buf, cursor::MoveTo(0, 0), terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
432	    let hdr = format!("  search: \"{}\"  {} result(s)  {}ms", query, groups.len(), ms);
433	    write!(buf, "\x1b[1m{}\x1b[0m", hdr.chars().take(w).collect::<String>())?;
434	
435	    // Pre-compute column widths across all groups for tabular alignment.
436	    let name_w   = groups.iter().map(|g| g.title.trim().chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0).min(45);
437	    let agent_w  = groups.iter().map(|g| g.agent.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);
438	    let branch_w = groups.iter().map(|g| g.branch.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0).min(30);
439	
440	    let mut screen_row = 1usize; // next terminal row to write (row 0 is title bar)
441	    let mut abs_row    = 0usize; // absolute content row (before scroll is applied)
442	
443	    for (gi, group) in groups.iter().enumerate() {
444	        if screen_row > content_h { break; }
445	        let selected = gi == sel;
446	
447	        // ── Session header ────────────────────────────────────────────────
448	        if abs_row >= scroll && screen_row <= content_h {
449	            let age     = age_secs_hit(&group.updated_at);
450	            let dot_col = match age {
451	                a if a < 900   => "38;5;82",
452	                a if a < 3_600 => "38;5;214",
453	                _              => "38;5;240",
454	            };
455	            let (name_col, meta_col, dot_char) = if group.backed_up {
456	                ("1;38;5;229", "38;5;240", "*")
457	            } else {
458	                ("38;5;242", "38;5;238", "·")
459	            };
460	
461	            let name: String = group.title.trim().chars().take(name_w).collect();
462	            let name_padded  = format!("{name:<name_w$}");
463	            let mut line = format!(
464	                "\x1b[{dot_col}m{dot_char}\x1b[0m \x1b[{name_col}m{name_padded}\x1b[0m  \x1b[38;5;240m{}\x1b[0m",
465	                group.dir,
466	            );
467	
468	            if branch_w > 0 {
469	                let branch_col = if group.backed_up { "38;5;75" } else { "38;5;239" };
470	                let b: String = group.branch.chars().take(branch_w).collect();
471	                let pad = " ".repeat(branch_w - b.chars().count());
472	                line.push_str(&format!("  \x1b[{branch_col}m{b}{pad}\x1b[0m"));
473	            }
474	
475	            if agent_w > 0 {
476	                let col = if group.backed_up { agent_color(&group.agent) } else { 239 };
477	                let a: String = group.agent.chars().take(agent_w).collect();
478	                let pad = " ".repeat(agent_w - a.chars().count());
479	                line.push_str(&format!("  \x1b[38;5;{col}m{a}{pad}\x1b[0m"));
480	            }
481	
482	            if let Some(sid) = &group.session_id {
483	                let id_short: String = sid.chars().take(8).collect();
484	                let ts = rel_time_hit(&group.updated_at);
485	                line.push_str(&format!("  \x1b[{meta_col}m{id_short}  {ts}\x1b[0m"));
486	            } else if matches!(group.kind, HitKind::Repo) {
487	                line.push_str(&format!("  \x1b[{meta_col}mrepo\x1b[0m"));
488	            }
489	
490	            render_row(&mut buf, &line, selected, SEL_BG, screen_row, w)?;
491	            screen_row += 1;
492	        }
493	        abs_row += 1;
494	
495	        // ── Excerpt rows ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
496	        for row in &group.rows {
497	            let ts_str = row.hit_ts.as_deref().map(rel_time_hit).unwrap_or_default();
498	            let n_lines = row.lines.len().max(1);
499	            let excerpt_indent = " ".repeat(2 + TS_W + 2);
500	
501	            for li in 0..n_lines {
502	                if abs_row >= scroll && screen_row <= content_h {
503	                    let text = row.lines.get(li).map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("");
504	                    let avail = w.saturating_sub(2 + TS_W + 2);
505	                    let text_t: String = text.chars().take(avail).collect();
506	
507	                    let exc_line = if li == 0 {
508	                        let ts_padded = format!("{ts_str:>TS_W$}");
509	                        format!("  \x1b[38;5;241m{ts_padded}\x1b[0m  \x1b[38;5;245m{text_t}\x1b[0m")
510	                    } else {
511	                        format!("{excerpt_indent}\x1b[38;5;245m{text_t}\x1b[0m")
512	                    };
513	
514	                    render_row(&mut buf, &exc_line, selected, SEL_BG, screen_row, w)?;
515	                    screen_row += 1;
516	                }
517	                abs_row += 1;
518	            }
519	        }
520	
521	        // Blank separator row between groups
522	        if abs_row >= scroll && screen_row <= content_h {
523	            queue!(buf, cursor::MoveTo(0, screen_row as u16), terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
524	            screen_row += 1;
525	        }
526	        abs_row += 1;
527	    }
528	
529	    // Clear any leftover rows below the results.
530	    while screen_row <= content_h {
531	        queue!(buf, cursor::MoveTo(0, screen_row as u16), terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
532	        screen_row += 1;
533	    }
534	
535	    // Status bar
536	    let bar = "  ↑↓/jk: navigate   space/→: open   q/esc: quit  ";
537	    let padded = format!("{:<width$}", bar.chars().take(w).collect::<String>(), width = w);
538	    queue!(buf, cursor::MoveTo(0, (h - 1) as u16))?;
539	    write!(buf, "\x1b[7m{padded}\x1b[0m")?;
540	
541	    stdout.write_all(&buf)?;
542	    stdout.flush()
543	}
544	
545	fn render_row(buf: &mut Vec<u8>, line: &str, selected: bool, bg: &str, row: usize, w: usize) -> io::Result<()> {
546	    use crossterm::queue;
547	    queue!(buf, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16), terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;
548	    if selected {
549	        let colored = with_bg(line, bg);
550	        let vis = visible_len(line);
551	        let pad = w.saturating_sub(vis);
552	        write!(buf, "\x1b[{bg}m{colored}{}\x1b[0m", " ".repeat(pad))?;
553	    } else {
554	        write!(buf, "{line}")?;
555	    }
556	    Ok(())
557	}
558	
559	// ── DB enrichment ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
560	
561	fn enrich_hits(hits: &mut Vec<SearchHit>) {
562	    let Ok(conn) = crate::db::connect() else { return };
563	    for hit in hits.iter_mut() {
564	        if let Some(sid) = &hit.session_id {
565	            if let Ok((agent, updated_at, remote)) = conn.query_row(
566	                "SELECT s.agent_name, s.updated_at, COALESCE(r.remote, '') \
567	                 FROM sessions s \
568	                 LEFT JOIN repositories r \
569	                   ON (s.cwd = r.directory OR s.cwd LIKE r.directory || '/%') \
570	                 WHERE s.session_id = ?1",
571	                [sid.as_str()],
572	                |row| Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, String>(1)?, row.get::<_, String>(2)?)),
573	            ) {
574	                hit.agent      = agent;
575	                hit.updated_at = updated_at;
576	                hit.backed_up  = true;
577	                hit.remote     = remote;
578	            }
579	        } else if let Some(dir) = &hit.repo_dir.clone() {
580	            if let Ok(remote) = conn.query_row(
581	                "SELECT COALESCE(remote, '') FROM repositories WHERE directory = ?1",
582	                [dir.as_str()],
583	                |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
584	            ) {
585	                hit.remote = remote;
586	            }
587	        }
588	    }
589	}
590	
591	// ── Direct name search ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
592	
593	fn search_sessions_by_name(query: &str) -> Vec<SearchHit> {
594	    let words: Vec<String> = query.split_whitespace().map(|w| w.to_lowercase()).collect();
595	    if words.is_empty() { return vec![]; }
596	
597	    let Ok(conn) = crate::db::connect() else { return vec![]; };
598	    let Ok(mut stmt) = conn.prepare(
599	        "SELECT session_id, session_name, cwd FROM sessions WHERE session_name != ''"
600	    ) else { return vec![]; };
601	
602	    stmt.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,String>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?)))
603	        .ok()
604	        .into_iter()
605	        .flatten()
606	        .flatten()
607	        .filter_map(|(session_id, session_name, cwd): (String, String, String)| {
608	            let normalized = session_name.to_lowercase().replace(['-', '_'], " ");
609	            if words.iter().all(|w| normalized.contains(w.as_str())) {
610	                Some(SearchHit {
611	                    kind: HitKind::Session,
612	                    title: session_name,
613	                    dir: short_path(&cwd),
614	                    excerpt_lines: vec![],
615	                    session_id: Some(session_id),
616	                    repo_dir: None,
617	                    start_ts: None, hit_ts: None, branch: String::new(),
618	                    agent: String::new(), backed_up: false, updated_at: String::new(),
619	                    remote: String::new(),
620	                })
621	            } else {
622	                None
623	            }
624	        })
625	        .collect()
626	}
627	
628	fn search_repos_by_name(query: &str) -> Vec<SearchHit> {
629	    let words: Vec<String> = query.split_whitespace().map(|w| w.to_lowercase()).collect();
630	    if words.is_empty() { return vec![]; }
631	
632	    let Ok(conn) = crate::db::connect() else { return vec![]; };
633	    let Ok(mut stmt) = conn.prepare(
634	        "SELECT name, directory, COALESCE(remote, '') FROM repositories WHERE name != ''"
635	    ) else { return vec![]; };
636	
637	    stmt.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,String>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?)))
638	        .ok()
639	        .into_iter()
640	        .flatten()
641	        .flatten()
642	        .filter_map(|(name, directory, remote): (String, String, String)| {
643	            let normalized = name.to_lowercase().replace(['-', '_'], " ");
644	            if words.iter().all(|w| normalized.contains(w.as_str())) {
645	                Some(SearchHit {
646	                    kind: HitKind::Repo,
647	                    title: name,
648	                    dir: short_path(&directory),
649	                    excerpt_lines: vec![],
650	                    session_id: None,
651	                    repo_dir: Some(directory),
652	                    start_ts: None, hit_ts: None, branch: String::new(),
653	                    agent: String::new(), backed_up: false, updated_at: String::new(),
654	                    remote,
655	                })
656	            } else {
657	                None
658	            }
659	        })
660	        .collect()
661	}
662	
663	// ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
664	
665	fn rel_time_hit(iso: &str) -> String {
666	    if iso.is_empty() { return String::new(); }
667	    let Ok(dt) = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(iso) else { return String::new(); };
668	    let secs = (Utc::now() - dt.with_timezone(&Utc)).num_seconds().max(0);
669	    if secs < 604_800 {
670	        match secs {
671	            s if s < 60     => "just now".into(),
672	            s if s < 3_600  => format!("{} min ago", s / 60),
673	            s if s < 86_400 => format!("{} hr ago",  s / 3_600),
674	            s               => format!("{} day{} ago", s / 86_400, if s / 86_400 == 1 { "" } else { "s" }),
675	        }
676	    } else {
677	        dt.with_timezone(&chrono::Local).format("%m/%d/%y").to_string()
678	    }
679	}
680	
681	fn age_secs_hit(iso: &str) -> i64 {
682	    if iso.is_empty() { return i64::MAX; }
683	    let Ok(dt) = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(iso) else { return i64::MAX; };
684	    (Utc::now() - dt.with_timezone(&Utc)).num_seconds().max(0)
685	}
686	
687	use super::agent_color;
688	
689	fn with_bg(s: &str, bg: &str) -> String {
690	    let reinsert = format!("\x1b[0m\x1b[{bg}m");
691	    format!("\x1b[{bg}m{}", s.replace("\x1b[0m", &reinsert))
692	}
693	
694	fn visible_len(s: &str) -> usize {
695	    let mut w = 0usize;
696	    let mut chars = s.chars().peekable();
697	    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
698	        if c == '\x1b' {
699	            for nc in chars.by_ref() { if nc.is_ascii_alphabetic() { break; } }
700	        } else {
701	            w += 1;
702	        }
703	    }
704	    w
705	}
706	
707	fn short_path(path: &str) -> String {
708	    let home = std::env::var("HOME").unwrap_or_default();
709	    if !home.is_empty() && path.starts_with(&home) {
710	        format!("~{}", &path[home.len()..])
711	    } else {
712	        path.to_string()
713	    }
714	}
```
