  "old_string": "fn draw_repos(\n    stdout: &mut impl Write,\n    repos: &[Repository],\n    sel: usize,\n    current_repo_dir: Option<&str>,\n    w: usize,\n    h: usize,\n    has_cd: bool,\n) -> io::Result<()> {\n    const SEL_BG: &str = \"48;5;236\";\n\n    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, 0))?;\n\n    let content_h = h.saturating_sub(1);\n    let mut row = 0usize;\n\n    let name_w = repos.iter().map(|r| r.name.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);\n    let dir_w  = repos.iter().map(|r| r.directory.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);\n\n    for (i, repo) in repos.iter().enumerate() {\n        if row >= content_h { break }\n\n        let is_sel = i == sel;\n        let is_cur = current_repo_dir == Some(repo.directory.as_str());\n        let dot_col = if is_cur { \"38;5;82\" } else { \"38;5;240\" };\n\n        let name_padded = format!(\"{:<name_w$}\", repo.name);\n        let dir_padded  = format!(\"{:<dir_w$}\",  repo.directory);\n        let line = format!(\n            \"\\x1b[{dot_col}m*\\x1b[0m \\x1b[38;5;255m{name_padded}\\x1b[0m  \\x1b[38;5;240m{dir_padded}  {}\\x1b[0m\",\n            repo.remote\n        );\n\n        print_row(stdout, &line, is_sel, SEL_BG, w, row as u16)?;\n        row += 1;\n    }",
  "new_string": "fn draw_repos(\n    stdout: &mut impl Write,\n    repos: &[Repository],\n    sel: usize,\n    current_repo_dir: Option<&str>,\n    w: usize,\n    h: usize,\n    has_cd: bool,\n) -> io::Result<()> {\n    let t = crate::theme::get();\n    let sel_bg = t.sel_bg;\n\n    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, 0))?;\n\n    let content_h = h.saturating_sub(1);\n    let mut row = 0usize;\n\n    let name_w = repos.iter().map(|r| r.name.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);\n    let dir_w  = repos.iter().map(|r| r.directory.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);\n\n    for (i, repo) in repos.iter().enumerate() {\n        if row >= content_h { break }\n\n        let is_sel = i == sel;\n        let is_cur = current_repo_dir == Some(repo.directory.as_str());\n        let dot_col = if is_cur { t.fresh } else { t.text_dim };\n\n        let name_padded = format!(\"{:<name_w$}\", repo.name);\n        let dir_padded  = format!(\"{:<dir_w$}\",  repo.directory);\n        let line = format!(\n            \"\\x1b[{dot_col}m*\\x1b[0m \\x1b[{pm}m{name_padded}\\x1b[0m  \\x1b[{dm}m{dir_padded}  {}\\x1b[0m\",\n            repo.remote, pm = t.text_primary, dm = t.text_dim,\n        );\n\n        print_row(stdout, &line, is_sel, sel_bg, w, row as u16)?;\n        row += 1;\n    }"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/status.rs",
  "old_string": "fn draw_sessions(\n    stdout: &mut impl Write,\n    repos: &[Repository],\n    repo_idx: usize,\n    sessions: &[RepoSession],\n    worktrees: &[RepoWorktree],\n    sel: usize,\n    w: usize,\n    h: usize,\n) -> io::Result<()> {\n    const SEL_BG: &str = \"48;5;236\";\n\n    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, 0))?;\n    write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[1;38;5;229m{}\\x1b[0m\", repos[repo_idx].name)?;\n    execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;\n\n    let content_h = h.saturating_sub(2); // header + status bar\n    let mut row = 1usize;\n\n    // ── Worktrees ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    if !worktrees.is_empty() {\n        for wt in worktrees.iter().take(content_h.saturating_sub(row + 1)) {\n            if row >= content_h { break; }\n\n            let (branch_col, branch_label) = if wt.branch == \"(detached)\" {\n                (\"38;5;196\", format!(\"detached:{}\", &wt.head))\n            } else if wt.is_main {\n                (\"38;5;229\", wt.branch.clone())   // gold — main worktree\n            } else {\n                (\"38;5;75\",  wt.branch.clone())   // blue — linked worktree\n            };\n\n            let path_short = short_path_s(&wt.path);\n            let line = format!(\n                \"\\x1b[38;5;240m  @ \\x1b[{branch_col}m{branch_label}\\x1b[0m  \\x1b[38;5;240m{path_short}\\x1b[0m\"\n            );",
  "new_string": "fn draw_sessions(\n    stdout: &mut impl Write,\n    repos: &[Repository],\n    repo_idx: usize,\n    sessions: &[RepoSession],\n    worktrees: &[RepoWorktree],\n    sel: usize,\n    w: usize,\n    h: usize,\n) -> io::Result<()> {\n    let t = crate::theme::get();\n    let sel_bg = t.sel_bg;\n\n    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, 0))?;\n    write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{hd}m{}\\x1b[0m\", repos[repo_idx].name, hd = t.header)?;\n    execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;\n\n    let content_h = h.saturating_sub(2); // header + status bar\n    let mut row = 1usize;\n\n    // ── Worktrees ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    if !worktrees.is_empty() {\n        for wt in worktrees.iter().take(content_h.saturating_sub(row + 1)) {\n            if row >= content_h { break; }\n\n            let (branch_col, branch_label) = if wt.branch == \"(detached)\" {\n                (t.error, format!(\"detached:{}\", &wt.head))\n            } else if wt.is_main {\n                (t.accent, wt.branch.clone())\n            } else {\n                (t.link, wt.branch.clone())\n            };\n\n            let path_short = short_path_s(&wt.path);\n            let line = format!(\n                \"\\x1b[{dm}m  @ \\x1b[{branch_col}m{branch_label}\\x1b[0m  \\x1b[{dm}m{path_short}\\x1b[0m\",\n                dm = t.text_dim,\n            );"
}

1].trim();\n                    if v.is_empty() { \"\\x1b[38;5;238m(skip)\\x1b[0m\".to_string() }\n                    else            { format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;255m{v}\\x1b[0m\") }\n                };\n                write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[38;5;240m{lpart}\\x1b[0m{val}\").ok();\n            } else if i == step {\n                // Active step\n                write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[38;5;255m{lpart}\\x1b[0m\").ok();\n                if i == 0 {\n                    // Inline agent picker\n                    for (ai, (name, _, col)) in AGENTS.iter().enumerate() {\n                        if ai > 0 { write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[38;5;238m │ \\x1b[0m\").ok(); }\n                        if ai == agent_sel {\n                            write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[48;5;236;38;5;{col}m {name} \\x1b[0m\").ok();\n                        } else {\n                            write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[38;5;240m {name}\\x1b[0m\").ok();\n                        }\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    // Text input — print typed chars, terminal cursor sits here\n                    write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[38;5;255m{}\\x1b[0m\", inputs[i -   "new_string": "fn new_session_wizard(stdout: &mut impl Write, repo_dir: &str, w: usize, h: usize) -> Option<NewSessionConfig> {\n    const LABEL_W: usize = 24;\n    // Panel rows: title, blank, agent, branch, name, prompt, blank  (7 rows + 1 status bar)\n    const PANEL_H: usize = 7;\n\n    let t = crate::theme::get();\n    let panel_top = h.saturating_sub(PANEL_H + 1);\n\n    let mut step = 0usize;           // 0=agent 1=branch 2=name 3=prompt\n    let mut agent_sel = 0usize;\n    let mut confirmed_agent = 0usize;\n    let mut inputs = [String::new(), String::new(), String::new()]; // branch, name, prompt\n\n    execute!(stdout, cursor::Show).ok();\n\n    loop {\n        // ── Draw panel ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n        // Title row\n        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, panel_top as u16)).ok();\n        write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{hd}m  New Session\\x1b[0m\", hd = t.header).ok();\n        execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine)).ok();\n\n        // Blank separator\n        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (panel_top + 1) as u16)).ok();\n        execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine)).ok();\n\n        let step_rows  = [panel_top + 2, panel_top + 3, panel_top + 4, panel_top + 5];\n        let step_labels = [\"  Agent\", \"  Worktree branch\", \"  Session name\", \"  Prompt\"];\n\n        for (i, (&row, &label)) in step_rows.iter().zip(step_labels.iter()).enumerate() {\n            execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16)).ok();\n            let lpart = format!(\"{label:<LABEL_W$}\");\n\n            if i < step {\n                // Completed — dim label, value in colour\n                let val = if i == 0 {\n                    let (name, _, col) = AGENTS[confirmed_agent];\n                    format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;{col}m{name}\\x1b[0m\")\n                } else {\n                    let v = inputs[i - 1].trim();\n                    if v.is_empty() { format!(\"\\x1b[{ft}m(skip)\\x1b[0m\", ft = t.text_faint) }\n                    else            { format!(\"\\x1b[{pm}m{v}\\x1b[0m\", pm = t.text_primary) }\n                };\n                write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{dm}m{lpart}\\x1b[0m{val}\", dm = t.text_dim).ok();\n            } else if i == step {\n                // Active step\n                write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{pm}m{lpart}\\x1b[0m\", pm = t.text_primary).ok();\n                if i == 0 {\n                    // Inline agent picker\n                    for (ai, (name, _, col)) in AGENTS.iter().enumerate() {\n                        if ai > 0 { write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{ft}m │ \\x1b[0m\", ft = t.text_faint).ok(); }\n                        if ai == agent_sel {\n                            write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{sb};38;5;{col}m {name} \\x1b[0m\", sb = t.sel_bg).ok();\n                        } else {\n                            write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{dm}m {name}\\x1b[0m\", dm = t.text_dim).ok();\n                        }\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    // Text input — print typed chars, terminal cursor sits here\n                    write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{pm}m{}\\x1b[0m\", inputs[i - 1], pm = t.text_primary).ok();\n                }\n            } else {\n                // Future step — very dim label only\n                write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{ft}m{lpart}\\x1b[0m\", ft = t.text_faint).ok();\n            }\n            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine)).ok();\n        }"
}

  "old_string": "fn draw(stdout: &mut impl Write, sessions: &[DisplaySession], sel: usize, w: usize, h: usize) -> io::Result<()> {\n    const SEL_BG: &str = \"48;5;236\";\n\n    let content_h = h.saturating_sub(1);\n    let scroll = if sel >= content_h { sel + 1 - content_h } else { 0 };\n\n    // Pre-compute column widths\n    let name_w = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.session_name.trim().chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0).min(40);\n    let cwd_w  = sessions.iter().map(|s| short_cwd(&s.cwd).chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);\n    let branch_w = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.branch.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);\n    let agent_w  = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.agent_name.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);\n\n    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, 0))?;\n\n    for row in 0..content_h {\n        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16))?;\n        let idx = scroll + row;\n        if idx >= sessions.len() {\n            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let s = &sessions[idx];\n        let is_sel = idx == sel;\n\n        let id_short: String = s.session_id.chars().take(8).collect();\n        let ts = relative_time(s.updated_at);\n        let age = (Utc::now() - s.updated_at).num_seconds().max(0);\n        let dot_col = match age {\n            a if a < 900   => \"38;5;82\",\n            a if a < 3_600 => \"38;5;214\",\n            _              => \"38;5;240\",\n        };\n        let (name_col, meta_col, dot_char) = if s.backed_up {\n            (\"1;38;5;255\", \"38;5;240\", \"*\")\n        } else {\n            (\"38;5;242\", \"38;5;238\", \"·\")\n        };\n        let branch_col = if s.backed_up { \"38;5;75\" } else { \"38;5;239\" };\n\n        let name: String = s.session_name.trim().chars().take(name_w).collect();\n        let name_padded = format!(\"{:<name_w$}\", name);\n\n        let cwd_short = short_cwd(&s.cwd);\n        let cwd_padded = format!(\"{:<cwd_w$}\", cwd_short);\n\n        let mut line = format!(\n            \"\\x1b[{dot_col}m{dot_char}\\x1b[0m \\x1b[{name_col}m{name_padded}\\x1b[0m  \\x1b[38;5;240m{cwd_padded}\\x1b[0m\"\n        );\n\n        if branch_w > 0 {\n            let b: String = s.branch.chars().take(branch_w).collect();\n            let pad = \" \".repeat(branch_w - b.chars().count());\n            line.push_str(&format!(\"  \\x1b[{branch_col}m{b}{pad}\\x1b[0m\"));\n        }\n\n        if agent_w > 0 {\n            let col = if s.backed_up { agent_color(&s.agent_name) } else { 239 };\n            let a: String = s.agent_name.chars().take(agent_w).collect();\n            let pad = \" \".repeat(agent_w - a.chars().count());\n            line.push_str(&format!(\"  \\x1b[38;5;{col}m{a}{pad}\\x1b[0m\"));\n        }\n\n        line.push_str(&format!(\"  \\x1b[{meta_col}m{id_short}  {ts}\\x1b[0m\"));\n\n        if is_sel {\n            let colored = with_bg(&line, SEL_BG);\n            let vis = visible_width(&line);\n            let pad = w.saturating_sub(vis);\n            write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{SEL_BG}m{colored}{}\\x1b[0m\", \" \".repeat(pad))?;\n        } else {\n            write!(stdout, \"{line}\")?;\n            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;\n        }\n    }\n\n    let bar = format!(\n        \"  {} sessions   ↑↓/jk navigate   space/enter: view   r: resume   q: quit  \",\n        sessions.len()\n    );\n    let display: String = bar.chars().take(w).collect();\n    let padded = format!(\"{:<width$}\", display, width = w);\n    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (h - 1) as u16))?;\n    write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[7m{padded}\\x1b[0m\")?;\n\n    stdout.flush()\n}",
  "new_string": "fn draw(stdout: &mut impl Write, sessions: &[DisplaySession], sel: usize, w: usize, h: usize) -> io::Result<()> {\n    let t = crate::theme::get();\n    let sel_bg = t.sel_bg;\n\n    let content_h = h.saturating_sub(1);\n    let scroll = if sel >= content_h { sel + 1 - content_h } else { 0 };\n\n    // Pre-compute column widths\n    let name_w = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.session_name.trim().chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0).min(40);\n    let cwd_w  = sessions.iter().map(|s| short_cwd(&s.cwd).chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);\n    let branch_w = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.branch.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);\n    let agent_w  = sessions.iter().map(|s| s.agent_name.chars().count()).max().unwrap_or(0);\n\n    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, 0))?;\n\n    for row in 0..content_h {\n        execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, row as u16))?;\n        let idx = scroll + row;\n        if idx >= sessions.len() {\n            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let s = &sessions[idx];\n        let is_sel = idx == sel;\n\n        let id_short: String = s.session_id.chars().take(8).collect();\n        let ts = relative_time(s.updated_at);\n        let age = (Utc::now() - s.updated_at).num_seconds().max(0);\n        let dot_col = match age {\n            a if a < 900   => t.fresh,\n            a if a < 3_600 => t.moderate,\n            _              => t.text_dim,\n        };\n        let (name_col, meta_col, dot_char) = if s.backed_up {\n            (t.backed_name, t.backed_meta, \"*\")\n        } else {\n            (t.unbacked_name, t.unbacked_meta, \"·\")\n        };\n        let branch_col = if s.backed_up { t.link } else { t.stale };\n\n        let name: String = s.session_name.trim().chars().take(name_w).collect();\n        let name_padded = format!(\"{:<name_w$}\", name);\n\n        let cwd_short = short_cwd(&s.cwd);\n        let cwd_padded = format!(\"{:<cwd_w$}\", cwd_short);\n\n        let mut line = format!(\n            \"\\x1b[{dot_col}m{dot_char}\\x1b[0m \\x1b[{name_col}m{name_padded}\\x1b[0m  \\x1b[{dm}m{cwd_padded}\\x1b[0m\",\n            dm = t.text_dim,\n        );\n\n        if branch_w > 0 {\n            let b: String = s.branch.chars().take(branch_w).collect();\n            let pad = \" \".repeat(branch_w - b.chars().count());\n            line.push_str(&format!(\"  \\x1b[{branch_col}m{b}{pad}\\x1b[0m\"));\n        }\n\n        if agent_w > 0 {\n            let col = if s.backed_up { agent_color(&s.agent_name) } else { t.stale_agent };\n            let a: String = s.agent_name.chars().take(agent_w).collect();\n            let pad = \" \".repeat(agent_w - a.chars().count());\n            line.push_str(&format!(\"  \\x1b[38;5;{col}m{a}{pad}\\x1b[0m\"));\n        }\n\n        line.push_str(&format!(\"  \\x1b[{meta_col}m{id_short}  {ts}\\x1b[0m\"));\n\n        if is_sel {\n            let colored = with_bg(&line, sel_bg);\n            let vis = visible_width(&line);\n            let pad = w.saturating_sub(vis);\n            write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[{sel_bg}m{colored}{}\\x1b[0m\", \" \".repeat(pad))?;\n        } else {\n            write!(stdout, \"{line}\")?;\n            execute!(stdout, terminal::Clear(ClearType::UntilNewLine))?;\n        }\n    }\n\n    let bar = format!(\n        \"  {} sessions   ↑↓/jk navigate   space/enter: view   r: resume   q: quit  \",\n        sessions.len()\n    );\n    let display: String = bar.chars().take(w).collect();\n    let padded = format!(\"{:<width$}\", display, width = w);\n    execute!(stdout, cursor::MoveTo(0, (h - 1) as u16))?;\n    write!(stdout, \"\\x1b[7m{padded}\\x1b[0m\")?;\n\n    stdout.flush()\n}"
}

  "old_string": "static MD_SKIN: OnceLock<termimad::MadSkin> = OnceLock::new();\n\nfn md_skin() -> &'static termimad::MadSkin {\n    MD_SKIN.get_or_init(|| {\n        use crossterm::style::{Attribute, Color};\n        let mut skin = termimad::MadSkin::default();\n        // Bright white bold\n        skin.bold.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(255));\n        skin.bold.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);\n        // Italic — subtle light color\n        skin.italic.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(252));\n        skin.italic.add_attr(Attribute::Italic);\n        // Inline code — cyan, no background\n        skin.inline_code.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(116));\n        skin.inline_code.object_style.background_color = None;\n        // Code blocks — similar cyan, no background\n        skin.code_block.compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(116));\n        skin.code_block.compound_style.object_style.background_color = None;\n        // Normal paragraph text — light grey\n        skin.paragraph.compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(252));\n        // Headers — bright\n        skin.headers[0].compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(229));\n        skin.headers[0].compound_style.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);\n        skin.headers[1].compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(222));\n        skin.headers[1].compound_style.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);\n        skin.headers[2].compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(216));\n        skin.headers[2].compound_style.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);\n        // Bullet list markers — same as body\n        skin.bullet.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(252));\n        skin\n    })\n}",
  "new_string": "static MD_SKIN: OnceLock<termimad::MadSkin> = OnceLock::new();\n\nfn md_skin() -> &'static termimad::MadSkin {\n    MD_SKIN.get_or_init(|| {\n        use crossterm::style::{Attribute, Color};\n        let t = crate::theme::get();\n        let mut skin = termimad::MadSkin::default();\n        skin.bold.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(t.md_bold));\n        skin.bold.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);\n        skin.italic.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(t.md_italic));\n        skin.italic.add_attr(Attribute::Italic);\n        skin.inline_code.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(t.md_code));\n        skin.inline_code.object_style.background_color = None;\n        skin.code_block.compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(t.md_code));\n        skin.code_block.compound_style.object_style.background_color = None;\n        skin.paragraph.compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(t.md_text));\n        skin.headers[0].compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(t.md_h1));\n        skin.headers[0].compound_style.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);\n        skin.headers[1].compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(t.md_h2));\n        skin.headers[1].compound_style.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);\n        skin.headers[2].compound_style.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(t.md_h3));\n        skin.headers[2].compound_style.add_attr(Attribute::Bold);\n        skin.bullet.set_fg(Color::AnsiValue(t.md_text));\n        skin\n    })\n}"
}

  "old_string": "fn render_card(card: &Card, width: usize, agent: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n    let mut lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    let w = width.saturating_sub(2);\n\n    match card {\n        Card::RepoLink { name, dir, branch } => {\n            let home = std::env::var(\"HOME\").unwrap_or_default();\n            let short = if !home.is_empty() && dir.starts_with(&home) {\n                format!(\"~{}\", &dir[home.len()..])\n            } else { dir.clone() };\n            let branch_part = if !branch.is_empty() {\n                format!(\"  \\x1b[38;5;220m[{branch}]\\x1b[0m\")\n            } else { String::new() };\n            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;240m▸ repo  \\x1b[0m\\x1b[1;38;5;75m{name}\\x1b[0m  \\x1b[38;5;240m{short}\\x1b[0m{branch_part}\"));\n        }\n        Card::Header { title, cwd: _, branch: _, ts, agent: hdr_agent } => {\n            let t = title.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"(untitled session)\");\n            let (agent_label, agent_col) = if hdr_agent.is_empty() {\n                (\"claude\".to_string(), 75u8)\n            } else {\n                (hdr_agent.to_lowercase(), agent_color(hdr_agent))\n            };\n            let agent_part = format!(\"  \\x1b[1;38;5;{agent_col}m{agent_label}\\x1b[0m\");\n            let ts_part = if !ts.is_empty() {\n                format!(\"  \\x1b[38;5;240m{}\\x1b[0m\", rel_time(ts))\n            } else { String::new() };\n            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[1;38;5;229m{t}\\x1b[0m{agent_part}{ts_part}\"));\n        }\n        Card::System { ts, subtype, content } => {\n            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;240m── {subtype}  {}\\x1b[0m\", rel_time(ts)));\n            lines.push(String::new());\n            for l in wrap(content, w) { lines.push(format!(\"  \\x1b[38;5;240m{l}\\x1b[0m\")); }\n                    }\n                    UserPart::ToolResult { name, content, is_error, .. } => {\n                        let col = if *is_error { \"38;5;196\" } else { \"38;5;177\" };\n                        lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{col}m  ◀ {name}\\x1b[0m\"));\n                        let visible: Vec<&str> = content.lines()\n                            .filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty()).take(8).collect();\n                        for l in wrap(&visible.join(\"\\n\"), w.saturating_sub(4)) {\n                            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;240m    {l}\\x1b[0m\"));\n                        }\n                        let total = content.lines().filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty()).count();\n                        if total > 8 {\n                            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[38;5;238m    … {} more lines\\x1b[0m\", total - 8));\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Card::AsstMsg { ts, parts } => {\n            let (agent_label, agent_col) = if agent.is_empty() {\n                (\"claude\".to_string(), 75u8)\n            } else {\n                (agent.to_lowercase(), agent_color(agent))\n            };\n            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[1;38;5;{agent_col}m── {agent_label}  \\x1b[0m\\x1b[38;5;240m{}\\x1b[0m\", rel_time(ts)));\n            for part in parts {\n                if let AsstPart::Text(text) = part {\n                    lines.push(String::new());\n                    for l in render_md(text, w.saturating_sub(2)) { lines.push(format!(\"  {l}\")); }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Card::ToolRound { .. } => {} // handled in build_flat\n    }\n\n    lines.push(String::new());\n    lines\n}",
  "new_string": "fn render_card(card: &Card, width: usize, agent: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n    let th = crate::theme::get();\n    let mut lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    let w = width.saturating_sub(2);\n\n    match card {\n        Card::RepoLink { name, dir, branch } => {\n            let home = std::env::var(\"HOME\").unwrap_or_default();\n            let short = if !home.is_empty() && dir.starts_with(&home) {\n                format!(\"~{}\", &dir[home.len()..])\n            } else { dir.clone() };\n            let branch_part = if !branch.is_empty() {\n                format!(\"  \\x1b[{lb}m[{branch}]\\x1b[0m\", lb = th.label)\n            } else { String::new() };\n            lines.push(format!(\n                \"\\x1b[{dm}m▸ repo  \\x1b[0m\\x1b[1;{lk}m{name}\\x1b[0m  \\x1b[{dm}m{short}\\x1b[0m{branch_part}\",\n                dm = th.text_dim, lk = th.link,\n            ));\n        }\n        Card::Header { title, cwd: _, branch: _, ts, agent: hdr_agent } => {\n            let title_str = title.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"(untitled session)\");\n            let (agent_label, agent_col) = if hdr_agent.is_empty() {\n                (\"claude\".to_string(), 75u8)\n            } else {\n                (hdr_agent.to_lowercase(), agent_color(hdr_agent))\n            };\n            let agent_part = format!(\"  \\x1b[1;38;5;{agent_col}m{agent_label}\\x1b[0m\");\n            let ts_part = if !ts.is_empty() {\n                format!(\"  \\x1b[{dm}m{}\\x1b[0m\", rel_time(ts), dm = th.text_dim)\n            } else { String::new() };\n            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{hd}m{title_str}\\x1b[0m{agent_part}{ts_part}\", hd = th.header));\n        }\n        Card::System { ts, subtype, content } => {\n            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{dm}m── {subtype}  {}\\x1b[0m\", rel_time(ts), dm = th.text_dim));\n            lines.push(String::new());\n            for l in wrap(content, w) { lines.push(format!(\"  \\x1b[{dm}m{l}\\x1b[0m\", dm = th.text_dim)); }\n        }\n        Card::UserMsg { ts, parts } => {\n            lines.push(format!(\n                \"\\x1b[1;{fr}m── user  \\x1b[0m\\x1b[{dm}m{}\\x1b[0m\",\n                rel_time(ts), fr = th.fresh, dm = th.text_dim,\n            ));\n            for part in parts {\n                match part {\n                    UserPart::Text(text) => {\n                        lines.push(String::new());\n                        for l in render_md(text, w.saturating_sub(2)) { lines.push(format!(\"  {l}\")); }\n                    }\n                    UserPart::ToolResult { name, content, is_error, .. } => {\n                        let col = if *is_error { th.error } else { th.tool_ok };\n                        lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{col}m  ◀ {name}\\x1b[0m\"));\n                        let visible: Vec<&str> = content.lines()\n                            .filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty()).take(8).collect();\n                        for l in wrap(&visible.join(\"\\n\"), w.saturating_sub(4)) {\n                            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{dm}m    {l}\\x1b[0m\", dm = th.text_dim));\n                        }\n                        let total = content.lines().filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty()).count();\n                        if total > 8 {\n                            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{ft}m    … {} more lines\\x1b[0m\", total - 8, ft = th.text_faint));\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Card::AsstMsg { ts, parts } => {\n            let (agent_label, agent_col) = if agent.is_empty() {\n                (\"claude\".to_string(), 75u8)\n            } else {\n                (agent.to_lowercase(), agent_color(agent))\n            };\n            lines.push(format!(\n                \"\\x1b[1;38;5;{agent_col}m── {agent_label}  \\x1b[0m\\x1b[{dm}m{}\\x1b[0m\",\n                rel_time(ts), dm = th.text_dim,\n            ));\n            for part in parts {\n                if let AsstPart::Text(text) = part {\n                    lines.push(String::new());\n                    for l in render_md(text, w.saturating_sub(2)) { lines.push(format!(\"  {l}\")); }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Card::ToolRound { .. } => {} // handled in build_flat\n    }\n\n    lines.push(String::new());\n    lines\n}"
}

mut is_first = true;\n            for (_, v) in obj.iter().take(4) {\n                let raw_val = if let Value::String(s) = v { s.clone() } else { v.to_string() };\n                let first_line = raw_val.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\");\n                let preview: String = first_line.chars().take(120).collect();\n                let suffix = if raw_val.lines().count() > 1 || first_line.chars().count() > 120 { \" …\" } else { \"\" };\n                let col = if is_first { t.text_primary } else { t.text_secondary };\n                lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{col}m    {preview}{suffix}\\x1b[0m\"));\n                is_first = false;\n            }\n            if obj.len() > 4 {\n                lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{ft}m    … {} more fields\\x1b[0m\", obj.len() - 4, ft = t.text_faint));\n            }\n        }\n        if let Some((content, is_error)) = result {\n            lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{ft}m  ────────────────────────────────────────\\x1b[0m\", ft = t.text_faint));\n            let col = if *is_error { t.error } else { t.text_dim };\n            let visible: Vec<&str> = content.lines()\n                .filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty()).take(8).collect();\n            for l in wrap(&visible.join(\"\\n\"), w.saturating_sub(4)) {\n                lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{col}m    {l}\\x1b[0m\"));\n            }\n            let total = content.lines().filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty()).count();\n            if total > 8 {\n                lines.push(format!(\"\\x1b[{ft}m    … {} more lines\\x1b[0m\", total - 8, ft = t.text_faint));\n            }\n        }\n        lines.push(String::new());\n    }\n    lines\n}"
}
```

row.lines.len().max(1);\n            let excerpt_indent = \" \".repeat(2 + TS_W + 2);\n\n            for li in 0..n_lines {\n                if abs_row >= scroll && screen_row <= content_h {\n > AGENT

Both theme modes work correctly. Here's a summary of what was done:

---

**What changed:**

New file `src/theme.rs` — a `Theme` struct with semantic color slots (25 fields) and two constructors `dark()` and `light()`. Detection runs once via `OnceLock::get_or_init`:
1. Checks `GOSSAMER_THEME=light|dark` env var (explicit override)
2. Checks `COLORFGBG` — if the background component is 7 (white) or 15 (bright white), uses light theme
3. Defaults to dark

**Light theme** uses colors visible on white/light backgrounds: near-black text (232), medium grays for metadata, dark green/amber/blue/red for status colors, and a light gray selection background (252).

**All 5 TUI command files updated** — `status.rs`, `sessions.rs`, `show.rs`, `search.rs`, `tidy.rs` — replacing every hardcoded `"38;5;N"` string and `const SEL_BG` with calls to `crate::theme::get().*`. The markdown skin in `show.rs` also reads from the theme.

To test: set `GOSSAMER_THEME=light` in your shell or terminal profile, or set it in your terminal app's config to export `COLORFGBG=15;15`.

> DEVELOPER

is this the idiomatic way to handle themes in the terminal? how does using the terminal's settings work?

> AGENT

Not quite — the current approach is incomplete. Here's what's actually happening in the terminal ecosystem:

## How terminal background detection actually works

### OSC 11 — the real standard

The proper way is to query the terminal directly by sending an escape sequence:

```
\x1b]11;?\x1b\\   →  terminal replies with:  \x1b]11;rgb:RRRR/GGGG/BBBB\x1b\\
```

Most modern terminals (kitty, Alacritty, iTerm2, xterm, anything VTE-based like GNOME Terminal) support this. You compute the luminance of the returned RGB and threshold it. This is what libraries like the Rust `termbg` crate implement.

The catch: you need raw mode to read the response before other input arrives, and you need a timeout for terminals that ignore the query (like tmux by default).

### `COLORFGBG` — unreliable

The env var we check is a remnant of rxvt and some old X11 terminals. **Most modern terminals don't set it** — Terminal.app, iTerm2, Alacritty, Warp, kitty all ignore it. So our current auto-detection only works if the user sets `GOSSAMER_THEME` explicitly.

### The honest landscape

There's no single idiomatic standard. What mature tools do:

| App | Approach |
|---|---|
| `bat`, `delta` | Explicit env var / config only |
| `helix` | OSC 11 query + explicit config |
| `vim`/`nvim` | `background` option (user sets it), or plugin does OSC 11 |
| Most TUI apps | Explicit config, default dark |

## What gossamer should do

Add the `termbg` crate — it handles OSC 11 with timeout, parsing, and `COLORFGBG` fallback in ~5 lines:

```toml
# Cargo.toml
termbg = "0.6"
```

```rust
fn detect() -> Theme {
    // Explicit override wins
    if let Ok(v) = std::env::var("GOSSAMER_THEME") { ... }

    // OSC 11 query — works on most modern terminals
    if let Ok(color) = termbg::rgb(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100)) {
        let luminance = 0.2126 * (color.r as f64 / 65535.0)
                      + 0.7152 * (color.g as f64 / 65535.0)
                      + 0.0722 * (color.b as f64 / 65535.0);
        return if luminance > 0.5 { light() } else { dark() };
    }

    dark()
}
```

Want me to add `termbg` as a dependency and wire it in? The existing `GOSSAMER_THEME` override stays — that's the right escape hatch for edge cases.

> DEVELOPER

Ok let's do that. are we using semantic names for colors? Use the standard 8 base colors: Utilize basic semantic names (Black, Red, Green, Yellow, Blue, Magenta, Cyan, White) and their "bright" variants

> AGENT

Good questions. Let me think through this carefully before writing any code.

**Two issues with the current approach:**

1. **Not semantic**: `"38;5;240"` tells you nothing. Even with termbg detection, we're choosing from 256 arbitrary colors. If the user has a Solarized or Nord terminal theme, those specific ANSI indices override the terminal's palette — the background reads OSC 11 but the text colors don't respect the theme.

2. **Partial benefit**: Using base-16 colors (30–37, 90–97, 40–47, 100–107) is fundamentally different — those 16 slots are *defined by the terminal's own color scheme*. A Solarized user gets Solarized green for `fresh`, a Nord user gets Nord green. The terminal's theme fully controls the palette.

**With base-16 colors, the themes simplify dramatically** — most values become identical between dark and light. Only a handful differ (text_primary, backed_name, and header which needs blue instead of yellow for contrast on white).

**Also fixing**: the `stale_agent` u8 field can be removed — just use `t.stale` inline. The MadSkin fields change from `u8` indices to `crossterm::style::Color` named values.

  "content": "use std::sync::OnceLock;\nuse std::time::Duration;\n\n// ── SGR parameter strings for the standard 16 ANSI colors ────────────────────\n// These map to whatever palette the user's terminal defines (Solarized, Nord,\n// Dracula, etc.) — the terminal theme controls the actual RGB values.\n\n// Foreground — standard\nconst BLACK:   &str = \"30\";\nconst RED:     &str = \"31\";\nconst GREEN:   &str = \"32\";\nconst YELLOW:  &str = \"33\";\nconst BLUE:    &str = \"34\";\nconst MAGENTA: &str = \"35\";\nconst CYAN:    &str = \"36\";\nconst WHITE:   &str = \"37\"; // color-7: \"gray\" in dark themes, base text in Solarized\n\n// Foreground — bright (\"intense\") variants\nconst BRIGHT_BLACK:   &str = \"90\"; // dark gray — universally readable as \"dim\"\nconst BRIGHT_GREEN:   &str = \"92\";\nconst BRIGHT_CYAN:    &str = \"96\";\nconst BRIGHT_WHITE:   &str = \"97\";\n\n// Background\nconst BG_BRIGHT_BLACK: &str = \"100\"; // dark gray bg — visible selection on both dark and light\n\n// Bold + color composites (bold attribute + standard color in one SGR string)\nconst BOLD_YELLOW: &str = \"1;33\";\nconst BOLD_BLUE:   &str = \"1;34\";\nconst BOLD_RED:    &str = \"1;31\";\nconst BOLD_WHITE:  &str = \"1;97\";\nconst BOLD_BLACK:  &str = \"1;30\";\n\n// ── Theme struct ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\npub struct Theme {\n    pub is_dark: bool,\n\n    /// Row selection background (\"48;5;N\" or base-16 bg code)\n    pub sel_bg: &'static str,\n\n    /// Metadata, timestamps, paths, decorative separators\n    pub text_dim: &'static str,\n    /// Very secondary (\"… N more lines\", non-backed meta)\n    pub text_faint: &'static str,\n    /// Normal readable foreground text\n    pub text_primary: &'static str,\n    /// Tool input secondary argument lines\n    pub text_secondary: &'static str,\n\n    /// Recent/active indicator — green\n    pub fresh: &'static str,\n    /// Moderate age / soft warning — yellow/amber\n    pub moderate: &'static str,\n    /// Inactive branch / stale state — gray\n    pub stale: &'static str,\n    /// Errors, detached HEAD — red\n    pub error: &'static str,\n\n    /// Bold section/page headers\n    pub header: &'static str,\n    /// Non-bold accent: main branch name\n    pub accent: &'static str,\n    /// Inline label color: branch tags, tool names\n    pub label: &'static str,\n    /// Linked worktrees, backed-up branches\n    pub link: &'static str,\n\n    /// Backed-up session name column (bold)\n    pub backed_name: &'static str,\n    /// Backed-up session metadata column\n    pub backed_meta: &'static str,\n    /// Non-backed session name column\n    pub unbacked_name: &'static str,\n    /// Non-backed session metadata column\n    pub unbacked_meta: &'static str,\n\n    /// Non-error tool result — magenta/purple\n    pub tool_ok: &'static str,\n    /// Bold tidy panel warning title — red\n    pub tidy_warn: &'static str,\n\n    // ── Markdown skin (crossterm Color, passed directly to MadSkin) ──────────\n    pub md_code: crossterm::style::Color,\n    pub md_text: crossterm::style::Color,\n    pub md_h1:   crossterm::style::Color,\n    pub md_h2:   crossterm::style::Color,\n    pub md_h3:   crossterm::style::Color,\n    pub md_bold: crossterm::style::Color,\n    pub md_italic: crossterm::style::Color,\n}\n\n// ── Palettes ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\npub fn dark() -> Theme {\n    use crossterm::style::Color;\n    Theme {\n        is_dark: true,\n        sel_bg:         BG_BRIGHT_BLACK,  // dark gray bg — subtle selection on dark terminal\n        text_dim:       BRIGHT_BLACK,     // gray — metadata, separators\n        text_faint:     BRIGHT_BLACK,     // same gray — \"N more lines\" etc.\n        text_primary:   BRIGHT_WHITE,     // near-white — primary readable text\n        text_secondary: WHITE,            // color-7 — slightly dimmer than primary\n        fresh:          BRIGHT_GREEN,     // vivid green — very recent\n        moderate:       YELLOW,           // yellow — moderate age\n        stale:          BRIGHT_BLACK,     // gray — inactive\n        error:          RED,              // red — detached, errors\n        header:         BOLD_YELLOW,      // bold yellow — section titles\n        accent:         YELLOW,           // yellow — main branch\n        label:          CYAN,             // cyan — inline labels, tool names\n        link:           BRIGHT_CYAN,      // bright cyan — linked worktrees, backed-up branches\n        backed_name:    BOLD_WHITE,       // bold bright white — prominent session name\n        backed_meta:    BRIGHT_BLACK,     // gray — id, timestamp\n        unbacked_name:  WHITE,            // color-7 — dimmer, not backed up\n        unbacked_meta:  BRIGHT_BLACK,     // gray\n        tool_ok:        MAGENTA,          // magenta — non-error tool result\n        tidy_warn:      \"1;91\",           // bold bright red — destructive action warning\n        md_code:        Color::Cyan,\n        md_text:        Color::Grey,\n        md_h1:          Color::Yellow,\n        md_h2:          Color::DarkYellow,\n        md_h3:          Color::DarkCyan,\n        md_bold:        Color::White,\n        md_italic:      Color::Grey,\n    }\n}\n\npub fn light() -> Theme {\n    use crossterm::style::Color;\n    Theme {\n        is_dark: false,\n        sel_bg:         BG_BRIGHT_BLACK,  // dark gray bg — clearly visible on light terminal\n        text_dim:       BRIGHT_BLACK,     // dark gray — same, readable on light bg\n        text_faint:     BRIGHT_BLACK,\n        text_primary:   BLACK,            // near-black — primary text on light bg\n        text_secondary: BRIGHT_BLACK,     // dark gray\n        fresh:          GREEN,            // dark green — more readable on light than bright green\n        moderate:       YELLOW,           // yellow — same\n        stale:          WHITE,            // color-7 — subtle on light\n        error:          RED,              // dark red — better contrast on light than bright red\n        header:         BOLD_BLUE,        // bold blue — yellow has poor contrast on white bg\n        accent:         BLUE,             // blue — main branch on light bg\n        label:          CYAN,             // cyan — same\n        link:           BLUE,             // blue — more readable than cyan on light bg\n        backed_name:    BOLD_BLACK,       // bold black — prominent on light bg\n        backed_meta:    BRIGHT_BLACK,     // dark gray\n        unbacked_name:  BRIGHT_BLACK,     // dark gray\n        unbacked_meta:  BRIGHT_BLACK,\n        tool_ok:        MAGENTA,          // same\n        tidy_warn:      BOLD_RED,         // bold red\n        md_code:        Color::DarkCyan,\n        md_text:        Color::Black,\n        md_h1:          Color::DarkBlue,\n        md_h2:          Color::DarkBlue,\n        md_h3:          Color::DarkCyan,\n        md_bold:        Color::Black,\n        md_italic:      Color::DarkGrey,\n    }\n}\n\n// ── Detection ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nstatic THEME: OnceLock<Theme> = OnceLock::new();\n\npub fn get() -> &'static Theme {\n    THEME.get_or_init(detect)\n}\n\nfn detect() -> Theme {\n    // 1. Explicit user override — always wins\n    if let Ok(v) = std::env::var(\"GOSSAMER_THEME\") {\n        match v.to_lowercase().as_str() {\n            \"light\" => return light(),\n            \"dark\"  => return dark(),\n            _ => {}\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. OSC 11 query — ask the terminal for its background color\n    //    termbg sends ESC ]11;?ST, reads the RGB response, and classifies it.\n    //    This works on most modern terminals (kitty, Alacritty, iTerm2, VTE, xterm).\n    //    Terminals that ignore OSC 11 cause a timeout; we fall through to step 3.\n    if let Ok(termbg::Theme::Light) = termbg::theme(Duration::from_millis(100)) {\n        return light();\n    }\n\n    // 3. COLORFGBG=\"fg;bg\" — set by some older terminals (rxvt, etc.)\n    //    Background index 7 (white) or 15 (bright white) → light theme.\n    if let Ok(v) = std::env::var(\"COLORFGBG\") {\n        if let Some(bg) = v.rsplit(';').next().and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<u8>().ok()) {\n            if bg == 7 || bg == 15 {\n                return light();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 4. Default to dark\n    dark()\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Each has the same pattern. The backed case uses 256-color `agent_color()` (keeps brand colors), the stale case uses `t.stale`. I need to split those into two branches:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Y9AiEt831vASZakXeQ8TXi
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/stephanostsoucas/p/gossamer/src/commands/status.rs",
  "old_string": "            if agent_w > 0 {\n                let col = if s.backed_up { agent_color(&s.agent) } else { t.stale_agent };\n                let a: String = s.agent.chars().take(agent_w).collect();\n                let pad = \" \".repeat(agent_w - a.chars().count());\n                line.push_str(&format!(\"  \\x1b[38;5;{col}m{a}{pad}\\x1b[0m\"));\n            }",
  "new_string": "            if agent_w > 0 {\n                let a: String = s.agent.chars().take(agent_w).collect();\n                let pad = \" \".repeat(agent_w - a.chars().count());\n                if s.backed_up {\n                    let col = agent_color(&s.agent);\n                    line.push_str(&format!(\"  \\x1b[38;5;{col}m{a}{pad}\\x1b[0m\"));\n                } else {\n                    line.push_str(&format!(\"  \\x1b[{st}m{a}{pad}\\x1b[0m\", st = t.stale));\n                }\n            }"
}
```
